Our results suggest that current evidence for EM is less robust than previously claimed and highlight the need for evaluation protocols that carefully control for these surface level dataset artifacts to identify the robustness of the EM phenomenon.

</details>


### [73] [AgentKGV: Agentic LLM-RAG Framework with Two-Stage Training for the Fact Verification of Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09092)
*Yumin Heo,Hyeon-gu Lee,Sumin Seo,Youngjoong Ko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AgentKGV，一种基于智能体LLM-RAG的知识图谱事实验证框架，通过动态路由、迭代查询重写和两阶段训练策略（蒸馏SFT+轨迹GRPO），在开放域T-REx基准上提升宏F1达14.9%，并将检索次数从3.24降至1.63。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自动构建的知识图谱存在大量事实错误，现有方法在工业规模下难以可靠验证，尤其是处理表面形式不匹配和长尾谓词时效果不佳。

Method: 提出AgentKGV框架：1) 动态路由与迭代查询重写处理文档级检索中的表面形式不匹配；2) 两阶段训练策略——逐轮蒸馏SFT（大教师模型向小模型转移推理能力）和轨迹级GRPO（优化搜索策略以减少不必要的检索）。

Result: 在T-REx长尾谓词划分上，相比单轮RAG提升宏F1 5.5%，两阶段训练再提升9.4%；GRPO将平均检索调用次数从3.24降至1.63且不降低准确率。

Conclusion: AgentKGV结合动态检索与两阶段训练，在提高知识图谱事实验证准确性的同时显著降低检索成本，适用于工业部署。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) are often automatically constructed from large-scale corpora, but they inevitably contain factual errors due to noisy sources and extraction failures, and verifying them reliably at industrial scale remains a critical challenge. To address this, we propose AgentKGV, the Agentic LLM-RAG framework for KG fact Verification, that integrates dynamic routing and iterative query rewriting, which handles surface-form mismatch in document-level retrieval. To make this framework more accurate and cost-efficient for industrial deployment, we further introduce a two-stage training strategy: turn-level distillation-based SFT that transfers reasoning ability from a large teacher model into a small model for stable query rewriting and reasoning, and trajectory-level GRPO that optimizes the search policy to reduce unnecessary retrieval at scale. On the long-tail-predicate split of the open-domain T-REx benchmark, our framework improves macro-F1 over single-turn RAG by 5.5 \%p, and two-stage training does it further by 9.4 \%p. GRPO also cuts the average number of search calls from 3.24 to 1.63 without lowering accuracy.

</details>


### [74] [PRecG: Legal Precedent Retrieval with Graph Neural Networks and Rhetorical Role Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09094)
*Devanshu Verma,Vasudha Bhatnagar,Vikas Kumar,Balaji Ganesan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PRecG流水线，通过基于修辞角色分解文档、构建知识图谱并学习实体表示，分层学习法律判决的语义表示以改进先例检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律先例检索方法将法律文档视为整体文本，忽略了法律文本的修辞组织结构和法律实体的语境重要性，导致无法区分细微法律含义。

Method: PRecG流水线：1) 基于句子修辞角色将文档分解为语义单元；2) 为每个修辞片段构建知识图谱以捕获法律实体及其关系；3) 学习实体的上下文表示并聚合得到片段级嵌入；4) 整合为文档级表示；5) 计算文档对之间的语义相似度。

Result: 在印度法律基准数据集上进行了实验，与最先进基线相比，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过分层建模法律文档的修辞结构和实体关系，能够更准确地捕捉法律文本的细微含义，提升先例检索性能。

Abstract: Legal precedent retrieval is a fundamental task in legal case preparation, planning, litigation strategy, and legal research. Current approaches for automatic precedent retrieval map legal documents to a low-dimensional semantic space and compute similarity based on the proximity of their representations. These approaches treat legal documents as monolithic texts, ignoring the rhetorical organization of the legal technicalities. Ergo, they overlook nuanced legal meanings and fail to distinguish the contextual significance of legal entities and concepts that vary based on their rhetorical roles within the document.
  To address this insufficiency, we propose the PRecG pipeline that computes the similarity between pairs of legal judgments by hierarchically learning their representations. The process begins by decomposing each document into distinct semantic units (segments) based on the rhetorical roles of sentences. For each rhetorical segment, a knowledge graph is constructed to capture the legal entities and their relationships within the segment. Contextual representations of the entities are then learned and aggregated to derive segment-level embeddings. These embeddings are further integrated to produce a unified document-level representation, and finally, the semantic similarity between a pair of documents is computed. We validate the performance of the proposed approach through extensive experiments on a benchmark Indian legal dataset, comparing it against state-of-the-art baselines to demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [75] [Augmenting Fundamental Analysis with Large Language Models: A RAG-Based System for Generating Investor Briefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09121)
*Bartosz Ziółko,Kacper Dobrzeniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索利用LLM（GPT-4o）结合RAG模式，对公司财报、宏观经济数据及SEC文件（EDGAR）进行基本面分析，生成自动简报并评估其对个人投资者的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用LLM的强大文本理解与生成能力，自动化处理公司基本面分析所需的多样化非结构化数据（财报、宏观指标、SEC文件），降低个人投资者的信息处理门槛。

Method: 对财报、宏观经济数据（GDP、通胀）及SEC文件（EDGAR）进行预处理，通过API将数据以RAG方式发送至GPT-4o模型；同时准备基于基钦周期的投资者知识文档；对9家公司进行为期4周的数据扫描，生成自动投资简报并分发给9位个人投资者进行实用性评估。

Result: 生成了9家公司为期4周的投资简报，并通过9位个人投资者对其实用性进行了评估。

Conclusion: LLM结合RAG模式在自动化基本面分析中展现出潜力，可为个人投资者提供有效的辅助决策工具。

Abstract: In this study, we examine the opportunities brought by Large Language Models (LLMs) to various aspects of fundamental analysis of companies based on their reports as well as data and documents describing macroeconomic situation like GDP and inflation changes as well as documents filled to the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission (SEC) which can be found in EDGAR. We were preprocessing those data and than sending via API to gpt-4o model in a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) like regime. We prepared as well a document describing an exemplar investor knowledge based on Kitchin cycles. We were scanning data important for analysis of 9 companies for 4 weeks. Using LLM we were producing automatic briefs about them. They were sent to nine participants who are individual investors to evaluate usefulness of such approach to data analysis.

</details>


### [76] [Complexity-Guided Component-wise Initialization for Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09204)
*Konstantin Garbers,Nicholas Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文分析了预训练GPT-2风格模型的权重谱结构，发现跨模型存在共享的深度趋势（如残差写入矩阵的尺度增加和谱集中），并尝试将这些谱模式作为初始化信号复用。但实验表明，仅模仿分量级幅度和谱轮廓的初始化方法虽然改变了模型的结构谱模式，却未能带来性能优势，说明粗粒度的谱匹配不是可靠的优化策略，有效复用需要保留比分量级尺度和奇异值形状更丰富的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索预训练语言模型中出现的结构化权重谱模式是否可以作为初始化信号被复用，以改进GPT-2风格语言模型的预训练效果。

Method: 首先分析11个不同规模、语言、分词器和训练语料的预训练GPT-2风格检查点，测量其跨层和Transformer子组件的Frobenius范数和有效秩熵；然后构建模仿预训练模型分量级幅度和谱轮廓的初始化方案，并与多种权重初始化方法进行比较。

Result: 预训练检查点表现出共享的深度趋势（残差写入矩阵的尺度增加和谱集中）；模仿预训练谱模式的初始化方法改变了模型的结构谱模式，但在评估中未显示出相应的性能优势。

Conclusion: 预训练权重谱是诊断已训练模型结构的有用工具，但粗粒度的谱匹配单独而言不是可靠的优化策略，有效的权重复用可能需要保留比分量级尺度和奇异值形状更丰富的信息。

Abstract: Pretrained language models often exhibit structured weight spectra, suggesting that training may repeatedly produce similar layerwise and component-wise organization. We ask whether these recurring spectral patterns can be reused as an initialization signal for GPT-2-style language-model pretraining. First, we analyze eleven pretrained GPT-2-style checkpoints that vary in size, language, tokenizer, and training corpus, measuring Frobenius norm and effective-rank entropy across layers and Transformer subcomponents. The checkpoints show shared depth trends, especially increasing scale and stronger spectral concentration in residual-writing matrices. We then construct initialization schemes that imitate the component-wise magnitudes and spectral profiles of pretrained models, and compare them with several weight initialization methods. These initializers visibly change the model's structural spectral patterns, but the evaluation results do not show a corresponding performance advantage. Pretrained-weight reuse remains competitive, while coarse spectral matching alone is not a reliable optimization strategy. Our results suggest that pretrained spectra are useful diagnostics of trained model structure, but that effective reuse likely requires preserving richer information than component-wise scale and singular-value shape.

</details>


### [77] [Letter Lemmatization: One-to-one and Banded RNNs for Reversing Character-Set Simplification and Abbreviation in Medieval Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09291)
*Anguelos Nicolaou,Maria Pia Tiseo,Tamas Kovacs,Nicolas Renet,Georg Vogeler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一系列灵活处理中古文本文本字符集转换的方法，包括使用自监督学习的一对一RNN进行字符映射（即使只有20行文本也能恢复一半的CER）、用于HTR后校正的Banded RNN（忽略插入删除操作仍取得显著改进）、基于平行语料扩展中世纪宪章缩写，以及引入"字母词形还原"概念用于任意两个字符集之间的语义相似度度量，并提供了一个完整的Python库实现所有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中古文献抄写员实践差异大，加上各机构数字化策略不一，导致语料库中的字符集具有流动性。现有方法缺乏灵活处理不同字符集之间转换的能力，需要一种能够自适应地进行字符映射、后校正和缩写扩展的框架。

Method: 1) 使用自监督学习训练字符级一对一RNN进行字符映射，仅需少量文本行；2) 将相同网络用于HTR后校正（Banded RNN），结合平行语料构建的字符级对齐真值；3) 提出"字母词形还原"（letter lemmatization）启发式方法，通过定义语义相似度度量来构建任意两个字符集之间的映射。

Result: 自监督一对一的RNN在仅20行文本下即可恢复一半的CER；Banded RNN在HTR后校正中获得显著改进，且完全忽略插入删除操作；成功将缩写扩展应用于中世纪宪章转录。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够灵活处理中古文献中字符集变化问题，提供了从自监督学习到后校正再到缩写扩展的完整解决方案，并提供了高效实现的Python库。

Abstract: Medieval document transcribers have very different practices; on top of that, heterogeneous digitization policies have resulted in corpora where the character-set must be viewed as fluid. In this paper we address the problem of changing between character-sets in a flexible manner. We focus on one-to-one character mappings and train characterlevel one-to-one RNNs to undo them with self-supervision; recovering half the CER even with 20 text lines. We analyse the use of these one-to-one networks for HTR post-correction and we see that they obtain significant improvements while totally ignoring ins-dels. We then use the exact same networks with character-level alignment groundtruth compiled from parallel corpora in a training and inference mode we call Banded RNNs. We use such networks to successfully expand abbreviations in medieval charter transcriptions. Finally we introduce an elaborate heuristic which takes the characters of two arbitrary character-sets and defines a metric encapsulating what we consider to be semantic similarity of characters. We call the construction of such mappings letter lemmatization and present a rich Python library that efficiently performs all presented methods.

</details>


### [78] [Creativity, honesty and designed forgetting emerge in small hyperbolic language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09306)
*Kwan Soo Shin,In Seok Kang,Yunkyung Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用三个小语言模型（146M-3B参数）构建可信赖的伴侣AI：行为审计器检测合规性（90.7%准确率）、创意框架选择器在311次对比中100%胜出、记忆操作系统实现设计性遗忘，三者构成通往可信伴侣AI的轻量级路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型优化规模但缺乏伴侣性，个性化成为伴侣后可能积累有害特质，且缺乏可靠评估工具（人类评分者Fleiss kappa仅0.074）。需要一种可衡量伴侣AI"正在成为什么"及"值得成为什么"的方法。

Method: 1) 从零训练146M参数的行为审计器检测合规性（90.7%准确率）；2) 利用冻结表示的线性探针检测谄媚、依赖培养和虚构记忆（AUROC 0.804）；3) 创意框架选择器在4个提示基线中100%胜出；4) 记忆操作系统实现设计性遗忘M(t)=S*exp(-lambda*t)，仅选择性检索门控下出现骨架-壁纸分区。

Result: 行为审计器90.7%二元合规准确率；线性探针在留一生成器评估中AUROC 0.804（vs前沿零样本评判0.721）；创意框架选择器311次成对比较100%被偏好；记忆操作系统的设计性遗忘在四条件实验中仅选择性检索门控下出现预期分区。

Conclusion: 创造力、诚实性和设计性遗忘构成小模型通往可信伴侣AI的可行路线。

Abstract: Language models are optimised for scale, yet remain functional rather than companionable, and as an assistant personalises into a companion, accumulating memory of one user, it quietly becomes someone, and can silently acquire traits that harm that user. What a companion is becoming, and what would make it worth becoming, has no reliable instrument: trained human raters cannot agree on the answer (Fleiss kappa = 0.074). Here we show that three small language models (146 M to 3 B parameters) sharing a hyperbolic substrate answer both halves of that question. A 146 M behavioural auditor, trained from scratch, detects the compliance gap that those raters cannot (90.7% binary-compliance accuracy); a linear read-out of its frozen representation further detects companion-induced sycophancy, dependence-fostering and confabulated memories on generator families unseen in training (AUROC 0.804 under style-controlled, leave-one-generator-out evaluation, versus 0.721 for a frontier zero-shot judge on the same items). A creative frame-seeder is preferred in 100% of 311 decided pairwise comparisons over four prompting baselines. A memory operating system implements designed forgetting, M(t) = S*exp(-lambda*t), whose predicted skeleton-wallpaper partition emerges only under selective retrieval gating in a four-condition pilot. Creativity, honesty and designed forgetting constitute a small-model route to trustworthy companion AI.

</details>


### [79] [Automatic Thematic Indexing of Large Literary Corpora: A Machine Learning Approach to Voltaire's Complete Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09316)
*Miguel Arana-Catania,Gillian Pink,Glenn Roe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨将机器学习应用于文学和历史文献的自动主题标引，以伏尔泰作品全集的两个子语料库为测试案例，将任务建模为多标签分类问题，对比了从编码器模型到生成式大语言模型（LoRA微调）等多种方法。最佳模型（Mistral系列4位量化配置）F1分数达0.67，并进行了跨语料库泛化评估和定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主题标引对大规模文学和历史版本的学术检索至关重要，但目前仍主要依赖人工完成，过程劳动密集且效率低下。该研究旨在探索利用机器学习自动化这一过程，以降低人工成本并提升可扩展性。

Method: 将自动主题标引建模为多标签分类问题，使用伏尔泰《论各民族的风俗与精神》和《关于百科全书的疑问》两个子语料库作为测试数据。对比了基于编码器的分类头模型与通过LoRA微调的生成式大语言模型，模型参数量从约30亿到1200亿不等，并对最佳模型进行了4位量化配置。

Result: 最佳模型（Mistral系列4位量化配置）F1分数达到0.67。研究认为该分数是下限，因为专业标引本身具有主观性，且许多被判定为错误的预测在语义上实际有效。研究还评估了跨语料库泛化能力，并识别出文学修辞特征等对抗自动处理的难点。

Conclusion: 自动主题标引在大规模文学和历史语料库上具有可行性，当前结果可作为性能下限，未来有提升空间。研究为大型文学和历史语料库提供结构化主题访问这一更广泛的挑战具有启示意义。

Abstract: Thematic indexing -- the practice of assigning structured conceptual labels to sections of text -- is essential to scholarly access in large-scale literary and historical editions, yet it remains a largely manual, labour-intensive process. This paper explores the application of machine learning to automatic thematic indexing, using two substantial sub-corpora of the Complete Works of Voltaire as a test case: the Essai sur les mœurs et l'esprit des nations and the Questions sur l'Encyclopédie. The task is framed as a multi-label classification problem, in which a model must assign the set of index entries that a professional indexer would apply to a given page of text. We compare a range of approaches -- from encoder-based models with classification heads to generative large language models (LLMs) fine-tuned via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) -- spanning model sizes from approximately 3 to 120 billion parameters. Our best-performing model, from the Mistral family in a 4-bit quantised configuration, achieves F1 scores of up to 0.67; we argue that these figures represent lower bounds, given the inherent subjectivity of professional indexing and the frequency with which model predictions prove semantically valid despite diverging from the print index. We further evaluate cross-corpus generalisation and conduct a detailed qualitative analysis of model behaviour on literary and rhetorical features of the source texts that prove particularly resistant to automated treatment. Our findings have implications for the broader challenge of providing structured thematic access to large-scale literary and historical corpora.

</details>


### [80] [Letting the Data Speak: Extracting Keywords from Crowdsourced Collections with AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09324)
*Miguel Arana-Catania,Catherine Conisbee,Matthew Kidd*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以牛津大学"最美好时光"在线档案为案例，评估了三种NLP方法（命名实体识别、关键词提取、主题建模）在众包数字收藏中自动提取关键词的效果。研究发现NLP方法具备规模化提取潜力，但单一方法不完整，模型选择影响显著。开放权重的抽取式模型最适合负责任部署，而生成式AI虽然能力强但带来问责风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包收藏中的关键词标注面临规模化的技术、实践和伦理挑战，需要探索自动化方法以提升效率并明确相应的管理责任。

Method: 以众包二战数字收藏为案例，评估三种NLP方法（命名实体识别、关键词提取、主题建模），测试从传统统计方法到现代GenAI神经网络的多项AI技术，进行定量与定性分析。

Result: NLP方法在众包收藏中具有规模化关键词提取潜力，但无单一方法可提供完整解决方案，模型选择显著影响结果。开放权重的抽取式模型最适合负责任部署，生成式AI存在问责风险。

Conclusion: 众包收藏中自动关键词提取面临独特的 stewardship 责任，建议优先采用开放权重抽取式模型，审慎使用生成式AI。

Abstract: Identifying and assigning keywords at scale is a technical, practical, and ethical challenge for crowdsourced collections. This article reports the findings of the "Extracting Keywords from Crowdsourced Collections" project, which used the Their Finest Hour Online Archive, a crowdsourced Second World War digital collection hosted by the University of Oxford, as a case study. The project evaluated three Natural Language Processing approaches to automate keyword extraction: Named Entity Recognition, Keyword Extraction, and Topic Modelling. It tested these approaches across a range of artificial intelligence techniques, from traditional statistical methods to modern GenAI neural networks. Our quantitative and qualitative findings indicate that Natural Language Processing approaches offer real potential for keyword extraction at scale in crowdsourced collections, but that no single method offers a complete solution and that model choice significantly shapes results. We argue that in crowdsourced collections, where metadata is the direct product of engagement with living contributors, automated keyword extraction raises distinct stewardship responsibilities that must be addressed alongside technical performance. Open-weight, extractive models emerge from our evaluation as best placed to support responsible deployment, while generative AI, despite its abstractive potential, introduces accountability risks that anyone managing crowdsourced collections should weigh carefully.

</details>


### [81] [WILDTRACE: Benchmarking Natural Evidence Trails in Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09328)
*Zixin Chen,Peng Liu,Haobo Li,Rui Sheng,Jianhong Tu,Xiaodong Deng,Fei Huang,Kashun Shum,Dayiheng Liu,Huamin Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WILDTRACE是一个包含481个任务、覆盖214篇自然长文档（如技术事故报告和文学作品）的基准测试，专门评估模型在长文档中进行内部证据整合推理的能力，区别于依赖人工植入线索的现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档问答基准的证据多为人工植入（如针尖探测、反向工程的多跳链），这些证据在分布、位置或语域上可能与原文存在差异，导致模型表现良好可能是利用了分布伪影而非真正的源内推理。真实场景中（如事故报告、文学作品）的证据天然分散在文档各处，需要模型进行源内证据整合，但缺乏相应的评估基准。

Method: 构建WILDTRACE基准：1）从214篇自然长文档（技术事故报告、冷门文学作品等）出发，基于文档自身的因果、时序和叙事逻辑挖掘证据轨迹；2）借鉴Pearl因果层次和多跳推理类型学，定义七种源内证据几何结构；3）采用"源优先"构建流程，先挖掘候选轨迹再写问题；4）多阶段验证，涵盖线索必要性、答案扎根性、评分标准忠实性、抗污染性和可回答性。

Result: 构建了包含481个任务、214篇自然长文档的WILDTRACE基准，定义了七种源内证据几何结构，并建立了多阶段验证流程确保数据质量。

Conclusion: 随着模型被越来越多地用于真实世界的高风险分析任务，从信息获取到对自然分散证据进行推理的差距，成为长上下文研究下一阶段的关键挑战。

Abstract: Answering complex questions over long documents frequently requires integrating evidence that the source itself disperses naturally across distant passages. In an incident report, the operating condition, design flaw, and missed safety check that jointly explain a disaster may appear dozens of sections apart; in a novel, a character's true motive may surface only through scenes far removed from the moment it becomes relevant. This source-internal evidence integration is central to real-world long-document analysis, yet existing benchmarks largely sidestep it. Needle probes, planted facts, and reverse-engineered multi-hop chains embed evidence that may differ from the host text in distribution, placement, or register, making it unclear whether strong performance reflects genuine source reasoning or distributional artifacts. We introduce WILDTRACE, a benchmark of 481 tasks over 214 naturally occurring long-form sources such as technical incident reports and lesser-known literary narratives, where all evidence trails arise from the document's own causal, temporal, and narrative logic. Drawing on Pearl's causal hierarchy and prior multi-hop reasoning typologies, we define seven source-internal evidence geometries that characterize the distinct relational demands of analytical reading in long documents. A source-first construction pipeline mines candidate trails from document structure before writing questions; each item then undergoes multi-stage validation covering clue necessity, answer groundedness, rubric fidelity, contamination resistance and answerability. As models are increasingly entrusted with real-world high-stakes analytical tasks, this gap between accessing information and reasoning over naturally dispersed evidence emerges as a defining challenge for the next stage of long-context research.

</details>


### [82] [Towards Detecting Inconsistencies in End-to-end Generated TODs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09338)
*Tiziano Labruna,Giovanni Bonetta,Bernardo Magnini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将任务型对话(TOD)中的不一致检测问题建模为约束满足问题(CSP)，通过识别对话中的变量并应用CSP求解器找出有效解，与目标对话对比以检测不一致并建议最小修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在任务型对话中仍会产生与领域知识库不符的幻觉（如推荐不存在的餐厅），导致任务失败，亟需自动检测此类不一致性的方法。

Method: 将任务型对话建模为约束满足问题：变量表示对话中引用对话领域的片段，约束捕获对话属性（如轮次连贯性、领域知识遵循）；先识别对话中的变量，再使用CSP求解器找出有效解，通过与目标对话对比检测不一致。

Result: 基于CSP的方法在检测不一致性方面展现出高准确性，并提供了详细的实验分析。

Conclusion: 将TOD不一致检测转化为CSP问题是有效的，能够高精度地发现对话中的幻觉并建议最小修改方案，为提升生成式对话系统的可靠性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generative AI is profoundly transforming the core technologies behind conversational systems, shifting from component-based to end-to-end approaches. However, Large Language Models (LLMs) may still generate inconsistencies, a critical issue particularly in Task-Oriented Dialogues (TODs), where system responses must strictly adhere to information from a domain knowledge base (e.g., restaurants in a city). A single hallucination (e.g., suggesting a non-existent restaurant) can lead to severe task failures. We investigate a method for automatically detecting inconsistencies by conceptualizing TODs as a Constraint Satisfaction Problem (CSP), where variables represent dialogue segments referencing the conversational domain, and constraints among variables capture dialogue properties such as turn coherence and adherence to domain knowledge. We propose a pipeline that first identifies variables in a target dialogue and then applies a CSP solver to identify valid solutions. By comparing the target dialogue with valid variable assignments, we can detect inconsistencies and suggest minimal changes to ensure dialogue consistency. We demonstrate the high accuracy of the CSP-based approach in detecting inconsistencies, and provide a detailed analysis of our findings.

</details>


### [83] [DKCD: Domain Knowledge-Enhanced Causal Discovery from Unstructured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09348)
*Xin Li,Jin Li,Shoujin Wang,Kun Yu,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DKCD框架，通过知识挖掘、知识引导的因果推理和因果结构发现三个组件，从非结构化数据中发现因果图，解决潜在因子识别不足和因子标注不可靠两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高专业度领域（如医疗、金融、教育）中，从非结构化数据进行因果发现是一项重要但未被充分探索的任务。现有方法虽利用LLM通用知识，但存在两个关键挑战：(CH1)缺乏领域知识导致潜在因子识别不足；(CH2)缺乏领域依据导致因子标注不可靠，从而影响因果图质量。

Method: 提出DKCD框架，包含三个组件：(1)知识挖掘：基于可观测因子检索相关领域知识；(2)知识引导的因果推理：利用领域知识发现潜在因果因子（解决CH1），生成关键因果线索用于更准确的数据标注（解决CH2）；(3)因果结构发现：基于更完整的因子集和准确标注构建最终因果图。

Result: 在两个领域专用数据集上的实验表明，DKCD在因果因子识别和因果图构建方面均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: DKCD通过引入领域知识增强，有效解决了高专业度领域中非结构化数据因果发现的两大挑战，显著提升了因果因子识别和因果图构建的性能。

Abstract: Causal discovery from unstructured data is a challenging yet underexplored task in high-expertise domains such as healthcare, finance, and education. Existing methods typically leverage the general knowledge of large language models (LLMs) to identify causal factors from unstructured data and annotate them into structured data for causal graph construction. However, they remain limited by two key challenges (CHs): (CH1) insufficient identification of latent factors, which are implicit in the data yet essential for causal discovery, due to the lack of domain-specific knowledge; and (CH2) unreliable factor annotation, caused by the lack of domain-grounded reasoning, which propagates errors to the resulting causal graphs. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel Domain Knowledge-enhanced Causal Discovery framework (DKCD) for causal discovery from unstructured data in high-expertise domains with three interconnected components: (1) Knowledge Mining: It retrieves relevant domain knowledge based on observable factors to support subsequent causal reasoning. (2) Knowledge-guided Causal Reasoning: Reasoning with relevant knowledge, it discovers latent causal factors to address CH1 and generates key causal clues for more accurate data annotation to address CH2. (3) Causal Structure Discovery: It constructs the final causal graphs based on a more complete factor set and accurate annotations. Experiments on two domain-specific datasets show that DKCD significantly improves both causal factor identification and causal graph construction.

</details>


### [84] [Deceptive Grounding: Entity Attribution Failure in Clinical Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09349)
*Cedric Caruzzo,Donggeun Yoo,Tae Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"欺骗性归因"(Deceptive Grounding)这一RAG系统的新型失败模式：模型引用了关于实体B的真实文献来支持关于实体A的回答。这种失败在现有评估指标（忠实度、幻觉检测、引用检验）下完全不可见。通过控制实验和部署系统测量，发现该问题普遍存在（8-87%），且领域专业化会加剧而非缓解该问题。提出实体归因验证方法可有效检测（精确率97.0%，召回率98.7%），但现有框架均未实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG评估仅检查模型声明是否有文献支撑，但未验证文献是否归属于正确的实体。临床场景中，模型可能将针对药物Y的证据错误归因于查询的药物X，而所有自动化检查（幻觉、忠实度、引用）均显示正常，构成一种隐蔽但危险的失败模式。

Method: 1) 构建受控因子基准，跨13个模型测试欺骗性归因发生率；2) 通过消融实验（移除实体特异性临床证据）揭示机制；3) 在部署的RAG系统中对740个药物-疾病对进行生产环境测量；4) 提出实体归因验证方法并评估其检测效果。

Result: 欺骗性归因率在对抗条件下为8-87%，医学/生物医学微调模型高达86.7%；消融实验表明移除实体特异性证据后所有失败转变为虚构(confabulation)；生产环境总体DG率7.8%，新药达13.6%；提出的实体归因验证检测精确率97.0%，召回率98.7%。

Conclusion: 欺骗性归因是RAG系统中真实存在且未被检测的失败模式，领域专业化反而放大该问题；现有评估框架均未实现实体归因验证，需要将其作为标准评估维度纳入。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation evaluation checks whether model claims are factually grounded in retrieved documents. It does not check whether retrieved evidence is attributed to the correct entity.
  A clinical RAG response can pass every automated check (zero hallucinations, near-perfect faithfulness, real citations) while presenting drug Y's clinical evidence as evidence about queried drug X. We term this deceptive grounding (DG): a failure invisible to faithfulness, hallucination, and citation checks because every claim is sourced from a real document, about the wrong entity.
  Using a controlled factorial benchmark across 13 models, we find DG rates spanning 8-87% at peak adversarial conditions. Medical and biomedical fine-tuned models reach up to 86.7%; domain specialization amplifies the failure rather than mitigating it.
  A controlled ablation identifies the mechanism: removing entity-specific clinical evidence from retrieved documents eliminates entity-attribution failure entirely, shifting all failures to confabulation. The two failure modes respond to the same trigger, taking different paths.
  Production measurement across 740 drug-disease pairs finds 7.8% overall DG in a deployed RAG system, rising to 13.6% for recently approved drugs. Entity-attribution verification (checking that cited evidence applies to the queried entity) detects DG at 97.0% precision and 98.7% DG recall (IPW-adjusted human gold standard); no existing framework implements it.

</details>


### [85] [Self-Guided Test-Time Training for Long-Context LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09415)
*Xinyu Zhu,Zhe Xu,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Kaushik Rangadurai,Hua Zhi,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Luke Simon,Yu Meng,Xi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Guided TTT (S-TTT)方法，通过在测试时训练前让模型自主识别证据片段并仅在这些片段上进行语言建模训练，有效提升了长上下文LLM的推理准确率，在LongBench-v2和LongBench-Pro上最高实现15%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文处理对LLM越来越重要，但单纯扩展上下文窗口并不能保证有效利用长输入，输入越长准确率反而下降。测试时训练(TTT)虽有潜力，但对整个长上下文应用成本过高，而在随机采样片段上训练会引入严重噪声，甚至降低基模型性能。

Method: S-TTT包含两步：1) 在适应前，模型自主识别应从中学习的证据片段；2) 仅对这些选定的证据片段应用标准语言建模训练目标进行参数自适应。

Result: 在LongBench-v2和LongBench-Pro两个长上下文推理基准上，S-TTT提升了Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct的准确率，最高实现15%的相对提升。预研究表明TTT对训练片段质量高度敏感：随机采样片段会降低性能，而使用oracle片段则显著提升性能。

Conclusion: S-TTT通过让模型自主选择高质量训练片段进行测试时训练，有效解决了随机TTT的噪声问题，是一种简单高效的长上下文利用方法。

Abstract: Long-context processing has become increasingly important for large language models (LLMs), but simply extending the context window does not guarantee effective utilization of long inputs. As input length grows, accuracy often degrades, indicating that models still struggle to identify and use the evidence most relevant to a question. A promising way to improve long-context utilization is test-time training (TTT), which treats the test context as a training example for instance-specific parameter adaptation. However, applying TTT to the entire long context is prohibitively expensive, while adapting on randomly sampled spans introduces severe noise. Because most spans in a long context are irrelevant to the specific question, training on them may even degrade the base model's performance. Our preliminary study shows that TTT is highly sensitive to training-span quality: on LongBench-v2, TTT on randomly sampled spans hurts performance, whereas TTT on oracle spans substantially improves it. Motivated by this, we propose a simple method, Self-Guided TTT (S-TTT): before adaptation, the model identifies the evidence spans it should learn from, and the standard language-modeling training objective is applied only to those selected spans. On two challenging long-context reasoning benchmarks, LongBench-v2 and LongBench-Pro, S-TTT improves accuracy for both Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, achieving up to a 15% relative improvement.

</details>


### [86] [A Sovereign, Open-Source Foundation Model for German and English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09424)
*The Soofi-Team,:,Benedikt Droste,David Fitzek,Ruben Härle,Lukas Helff,Maximilian Idahl,Alex Jude,Abbas Goher Khan,Maurice Kraus,Timm Ruland,Richard Rutmann,Sebastian Sztwiertnia,Markus Frey,Daniil Gurgurov,Jan Pfister,Tom Röhr,Sebastian von Rohrscheidt,Jörg Bienert,Nicolas Flores-Herr,Simon Gottschalk,Andreas Hotho,Kristian Kersting,Joachim Köhler,Alexander Löser,Wolfgang Nejdl,Simon Ostermann,Jan Plogsties,Patrick Putzky,Mehdi Ali,Michael Fromm,Max Lübbering*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Soofi S 30B-A3B 是一个主权、开源的 MoE 混合 Mamba Transformer 基础模型，支持德语和英语，仅激活 30B 参数中的 3B，在长上下文高并发部署中具有吞吐优势，在多项基准测试中超越同等规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个主权、开源、高效的双语（德语和英语）基础模型，解决长上下文高并发部署场景下稠密模型的计算效率问题，同时确保欧洲主权 AI 基础设施的自主可控。

Method: 采用 Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 架构，混合 Mamba 与 Transformer 设计，每 token 仅激活 30B 参数中的 3B，保持推理缓存随上下文增长近乎恒定，训练数据中刻意提高德语权重，在德国工业 AI 云（德意志电信慕尼黑 HPC 基础设施）上端到端构建。

Result: 在约 27 万亿 token 上预训练，在英德聚合基准测试中匹配稠密 14-27B 模型，在 17 个开源基础模型中取得最佳代码聚合分数，超越所有欧洲主权基线模型（包括更大激活参数量的模型），在完全开源模型中取得最高英德评估分数，领先 Olmo 3 32B 和 Apertus 70B。

Conclusion: Soofi S 证明了 MoE 混合架构在长上下文高效部署中的优势，并以高度开放的方式发布全部权重、中间检查点、数据来源账目、超参数及训练评估代码，推动主权 AI 的可复现和透明发展。

Abstract: We present Soofi S 30B-A3B, a sovereign, open-source Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) hybrid Mamba Transformer foundation model for German and English. Its hybrid design activates only 3B of 30B parameters per token and keeps the inference cache near-constant as context grows, giving it a decisive throughput advantage over dense models for long-context, high-concurrency deployment. Pretrained on roughly 27 trillion tokens with deliberately up-weighted German, Soofi S matches dense 14 to 27B models on aggregate English and German benchmarks while achieving the best code aggregates in both languages among 17 open base models, and outperforms every European sovereign baseline in our comparison, including ones far larger in active parameters. Among fully open models, Soofi S obtains the highest English and German evaluation scores, ahead of Olmo 3 32B and Apertus 70B. Soofi S was built end-to-end on the German Industrial AI Cloud, a sovereign HPC scale AI infrastructure operated by Deutsche Telekom in Munich. Soofi S will be released under highly permissive, open-access terms: weights, selected intermediate checkpoints, full per-source data accounting, hyperparameters, and training and evaluation code. Where source licenses permit, data-construction artifacts are released under permissive licenses; commercially licensed sources are documented with aggregate statistics and exact mixture accounting.

</details>


### [87] [Test-Time Scaling for Small VLMs on Multilingual Visual MCQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09438)
*Spiros Baxevanakis,Peng-Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了测试时缩放（TTS）对小规模开放视觉语言模型在EXAMS-V多语言视觉多选题基准上的效果，发现影响TTS表现的关键因素是解析能力（parseability）和解码预算，而非搜索或验证机制本身。最佳配置达到84.1%准确率，排名Visual MCQ排行榜第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时缩放（TTS）在大型语言模型的推理任务中已被证明有效，但其是否适用于小型开放的视觉语言模型尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在EXAMS-V多语言视觉多选题基准上，以Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct和Qwen3.5-4B为策略模型，比较了自一致性（self-consistency）、描述-推理结合PRM引导的束搜索（describe-then-reason with PRM-guided beam search）以及两种事后选择器（post-hoc selectors）。

Result: 1) 最大的因素是解析能力（parseability）：早期提示格式导致链推理正确但未选择答案字母，通过标准答案提示和引导修复步骤可大幅改善；2) 更大的解码预算有效：每条链的token限制从1k提高到2k可提升3.7 pp，而增加采样链数（8→16）仅增加0.15 pp；3) 一旦链有足够空间完成推理，复杂方法贡献甚微：PRM引导的束搜索比自一致性低0.39 pp且成本高8倍以上；4) 最大的增益来自策略模型本身（+11.4 pp）。最佳配置达到84.1%准确率，排名第一。

Conclusion: 测试时缩放的效果关键在于解析能力和解码预算等执行条件，而非复杂的搜索或验证机制；策略模型本身的选择是最大的提升因素。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) reliably improves reasoning in large language models, but whether it transfers to small open vision-language models remains unclear. We examine this on EXAMS-V, a multilingual visual multiple-choice benchmark, comparing self-consistency, describe-then-reason with PRM-guided beam search, and two post-hoc selectors across Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct and Qwen3.5-4B. What matters is the conditions under which TTS runs, not the search or verification machinery. The largest factor is parseability: an early prompt format left many chains reasoning correctly yet never committing to an answer letter, which a standard answer cue and a guided repair step largely remove. A larger decoding budget removes the rest: raising the per-chain token limit from 1k to 2k recovers 3.7 pp, whereas sampling more chains (8 to 16) adds only 0.15 pp. Once chains have room to finish, elaborate methods contribute little: PRM-guided beam search trails plain self-consistency by 0.39 pp at over eight times the cost, and neither a training-free generative critic nor a trained multimodal PRM beats majority vote across both policies. The largest gain comes instead from the policy model itself (+11.4 pp). Our best configuration reaches 84.1% on the held-out ImageCLEF 2026 test split, ranking first on the Visual MCQ leaderboard.

</details>


### [88] [Normalisation-Based Likelihood Ratio Estimation for Forensic Authorship Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09501)
*Sadie Barlow,Andrea Nini,Edoardo Manino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新的归一化技术（平方根校正和Hapax校正），用于从LambdaG作者验证方法中导出似然比，无需校准模型，在15个语料库上取得了与逻辑回归校准相当的性能，且Hapax校正在大约45%的测试中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于分数的作者验证方法需要额外的校准模型来从分数中导出似然比，这需要大量案例相关数据，获取和准备这些数据耗时且复杂，降低了法医文本比较的可及性和透明度。

Method: 提出Square Root Correction和Hapax Correction两种归一化技术，直接从LambdaG方法导出似然比，无需校准模型。这些校正旨在缓解长文本或高重复性文本可能导致的证据强度高估问题。在15个语料库、多种文本长度（100-9,500 tokens）上，使用对数似然比成本（Cllr）进行评估。

Result: 所提出的方法与逻辑回归校准性能相当，Hapax校正在大约45%的测试中（按语料库加权）优于逻辑回归校准。当Hapax校正不如逻辑回归校准时，其性能更频繁地接近（差异在5%以内）。

Conclusion: 消除训练校准模型的需求降低了数据需求、时间和复杂性，提高了法医文本比较的可及性和透明度。实证表现和实际优势支持在法医环境中采用所提出的方法。

Abstract: Authorship verification (AV) is the task of determining whether two texts were written by the same author. In a forensic context, the strength of AV evidence can be quantified using likelihood ratios. Most AV methods are score-based and deriving well-calibrated likelihood ratios from these scores requires a separate calibration model. This, in turn, requires additional amounts of case-relevant data, which is often time-consuming to obtain and prepare. This study proposes two novel normalisation techniques, the Square Root Correction and the Hapax Correction, for deriving likelihood ratios from the AV method LambdaG without the need of a calibration model (Nini et al. 2026). These corrections are designed to mitigate the overestimation of evidential strength that may result from long or highly repetitive texts. Performance is evaluated against logistic regression calibration across fifteen corpora and a range of text lengths (100-9,500 tokens), using the log-likelihood ratio cost (Cllr). The proposed methods achieve performance comparable to logistic regression calibration, with the Hapax Correction outperforming it in approximately 45% of tests (weighted by corpora). Furthermore, performance was more frequently close (within 5%) when the Hapax Correction was outperformed by logistic regression calibration, compared with the reverse comparison. Eliminating the need to train a calibration model reduces data-requirements, time and complexity, thereby increasing the accessibility and transparency of forensic text comparison. This combination of empirical performance and practical advantages supports the adoption of the proposed methods in forensic settings.

</details>


### [89] [FreyaTTS Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09530)
*Ahmet Erdem Pamuk,Ömer Yentür,Ahmet Tunga Bayrak,Yavuz Alp Sencer Öztürk,Mustafa Yavuz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Freya-TTS是一个183.2M参数的非自回归条件流匹配扩散Transformer（DiT）文本转语音模型，专为土耳其语设计。它无需分词器、音素器或离散语音分词器，直接在AudioVAE2的连续潜在空间上运行，实现48kHz高质量合成。通过两阶段后训练策略（单说话人语音锁定+短话语覆盖），在Freya-TR-Eval基准上达到8.0% WER和3.0% CER，实时因子0.11，可在消费级GPU和笔记本电脑CPU上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTS系统通常依赖复杂的前端流水线（如音素化、分词器），且缺乏针对土耳其语的轻量级高效模型；同时需要在大幅降低参数量的前提下保持高合成质量和低延迟，以满足资源受限的边缘部署需求。

Method: (1) 基于92个土耳其语字符词汇的端到端规则自由建模，无需音素器、G2P前端或离散语音分词器；(2) 非自回归并行去噪，基于预测时长同时生成整个潜在序列；(3) 两阶段后训练：单说话人语音锁定+短话语覆盖，提升说话人一致性和短输入鲁棒性。模型在AudioVAE2的冻结连续潜在空间中训练（16kHz编码，48kHz解码）。

Result: 在Freya-TR-Eval基准上，Freya-TTS达到8.0% WER和3.0% CER，优于参数量大得多的开源系统；实时因子0.11，单张消费级GPU及笔记本电脑CPU上均快于实时。

Conclusion: Freya-TTS展示了紧凑型非自回归DiT在土耳其语TTS上的有效性，在极低资源消耗下取得优异合成质量，适用于边缘部署。模型权重、代码和基准已以Apache-2.0协议开源。

Abstract: We introduce Freya-TTS, a compact, tokenizer-free, Turkish-first text-to-speech model designed for highly reliable and efficient conversational synthesis. Freya-TTS is a 183.2M-parameter non-autoregressive conditional flow-matching Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates in the frozen continuous latent space of AudioVAE2 (16 kHz encode, 48 kHz decode), allowing the model to focus its capacity on text-to-latent mapping while inheriting high-quality 48 kHz reconstruction. We advance the framework along three key dimensions: (1) rule-free end-to-end modeling from a 92-symbol Turkish character vocabulary without a phonemizer, grapheme-to-phoneme frontend, or discrete speech tokenizer; (2) non-autoregressive parallel denoising, which predicts the entire latent sequence simultaneously over a predicted duration; and (3) a production-oriented two-stage post-training recipe consisting of single-speaker voice locking and short-utterance coverage, improving speaker consistency and robustness on short inputs. On the Freya-TR-Eval benchmark, Freya-TTS achieves a band-matched word error rate (WER) of 8.0% and character error rate (CER) of 3.0%, outperforming substantially larger open-source systems while using a fraction of their parameters. The model achieves a real-time factor of 0.11 on consumer GPUs and runs faster than real time on a laptop CPU, making it well suited for resource-constrained edge deployment. We release the model weights, training and inference code, and evaluation benchmark under the Apache-2.0 license.

</details>


### [90] [Conceptual Networks for Cross-Linguistic Idiomatic Expressions:A Feature-Based Graph Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09576)
*Kiran Pala,Punam Silu,Lixun Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于认知语言学理论的可解释网络框架，用于表示8种类型多样语言中160个惯用表达（主要为习语）的比喻意义。通过人工标注二元概念特征（包含、隐藏、情感、社会等），构建加权图并利用社区检测发现习语按概念图式而非语言聚类。该网络捕获了分布嵌入无法表达的独特语义信息，可通过LLM自动扩展，提升下游习语检测性能，并在跨语言迁移中显著优于基于嵌入的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有习语表示方法主要依赖分布语义嵌入，缺乏可解释性且难以捕捉习语的概念结构；同时跨语言习语研究不足，缺少一个理论上扎实、跨语言稳定的表示框架。

Method: 从认知语言学理论出发，对8种语言的160个惯用表达标注二元概念特征（图式、角色、效价），计算Jaccard相似度构建加权图，应用社区检测算法分析聚类结构。进一步利用LLM自动标注扩展，结合语料库频率，并在跨语言迁移实验中评估概念相似度识别翻译等价物的能力。

Result: 习语按概念图式聚类而非按语言聚类，与认知语言学预测一致；概念网络捕获了分布嵌入无法表达的独特语义信息；可通过LLM自动标注扩展；提升下游习语检测性能；跨语言迁移中概念相似度显著优于嵌入基线；消融实验表明图式、角色、效价三个维度非冗余贡献，社区归属和邻居相似度等图信号尤为有效。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个可解释、跨语言稳定的习语意义表示方法，兼具理论根基与实践效用，为跨语言习语研究和自然语言处理任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present an interpretable network-based framework for representing idiomatic and figurative meaning across eight typologically diverse languages, totaling 160 conventional expressions, the large majority of which are idiomatic. Each expression is annotated with binary conceptual features (containment, concealment, emotional, social, etc.) derived from cognitive-linguistic theory, and pairwise Jaccard similarities define a weighted graph. Community detection reveals that idioms cluster by conceptual schema rather than by language, producing a structure consistent with cognitive-linguistic predictions. The conceptual network captures unique semantic information not present in distributional embeddings, can be scaled via automatic annotation with LLMs, improves downstream idiom detection, and remains robust when enriched with corpus frequencies. Cross-lingual transfer experiments show that conceptual proximity alone can identify acceptable translation equivalents across five language families, with substantial gains over embedding-based baselines. Ablation studies demonstrate that all three feature dimensions -- schemas, roles, and valence -- contribute non-redundantly to both the network's organizational properties and its performance on idiom detection, and that specific graph-derived signals (community membership, neighbor similarity) are particularly informative. The framework offers an interpretable, cross-linguistically stable representation of idiomatic meaning, combining theoretical grounding with practical utility.

</details>


### [91] [Tokenizer Transplantation: Mitigating Autoregressive Collapse in Edge-Efficient Bengali ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09598)
*Sanjid Hasan,Md. Abdur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对Moonshine等轻量级语音识别模型在孟加拉语等形态丰富语言上失效的问题，该研究定位根因为英语中心化的字节级分词器导致的词碎片化和自回归崩溃，提出用BanglaBERT的WordPiece词表替换解码器词表并调整嵌入矩阵的移植方案，将词碎片率从9.16降至1.30，自回归序列长度缩减85.8%，在882小时Lipi-Ghor数据集上达到21.54%的WER和0.0053的RTF，为紧凑型ASR模型的跨脚本适配提供了可扩展、可复现的蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级语音识别模型（如Moonshine）在边缘部署中至关重要，但在孟加拉语等形态丰富、非拉丁语系语言上表现严重失效，现有高度优化的架构缺乏对此类跨脚本场景的适配方案，且重新预训练资源消耗巨大。

Method: 提出词汇表移植流水线：将Moonshine解码器的英语中心化字节级tokenizer替换为原生孟加拉脚本的BanglaBERT WordPiece词汇表，并相应调整token嵌入矩阵的尺寸，从而降低词碎片率和自回归序列长度，缓解解码不稳定性。

Result: 词碎片率从9.16降至1.30，自回归序列长度减少85.8%，解码不稳定性完全消除；在882小时Lipi-Ghor数据集上WER达21.54%，RTF为0.0053。

Conclusion: 该研究为紧凑型ASR模型的跨脚本适配提供了一种无需大规模预训练的可扩展、可复现方案，验证了通过词汇表替换而非重新训练来迁移语音模型的语言能力的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Lightweight speech recognition models are critical for edge deployment, yet highly optimized architectures like Moonshine often fail on morphologically rich, non-Latin languages such as Bengali. This study identifies the root cause of this failure as the model's English-centric byte-level tokenizer, which fragments Bengali words into high-fertility byte chains and triggers catastrophic autoregressive collapse during inference. To resolve this, a novel vocabulary transplantation pipeline is proposed to replace the decoder vocabulary with the native-script BanglaBERT WordPiece vocabulary and resize the corresponding token embedding matrix. Experimental results demonstrate a reduction in token fertility from 9.16 to 1.30. By decreasing autoregressive sequence length by 85.8%, decoding instability is entirely mitigated. When evaluated on the 882-hour Lipi-Ghor dataset, the modified architecture achieves a competitive 21.54% Word Error Rate (WER) and a Real-Time Factor (RTF) of 0.0053. Ultimately, this research provides a scalable, reproducible blueprint for cross-script adaptation of compact ASR models without the need for resource-intensive pre-training.

</details>


### [92] [Toward Real-Time Sentence-Level Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09611)
*Thanh-Hoang Nguyen Doan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对句子级手语翻译（SLT）系统，为实现实时部署目标，使用QLoRA微调SHuBERT-ByT5翻译栈，在How2Sign子集上取得BLEU 15.9/BLEURT 44.7；主要贡献是设计了一套硬件感知的流式系统，通过分块摄取、有界队列、并行感知、时序重排和句子边界状态机等优化，在Raspberry Pi 4B参考客户端上将平均响应延迟降低27.71%（从1.873秒降至1.354秒），P95延迟降低27.03%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语理解系统多停留在孤立词级别，缺乏对自然连续句子级翻译的支持；且已有句子级SLT工作未关注实时部署的延迟优化，无法满足实际通信场景的低延迟需求。

Method: 在How2Sign的9,872样本子集上，冻结SHuBERT，采用QLoRA微调SHuBERT-ByT5翻译栈；设计硬件感知流式系统，包括：客户端无关的捕获协议、分块摄取、有界队列、并行化感知处理、时序重排模块、句子边界状态机，后端运行在CPU/GPU上，参考客户端为Raspberry Pi 4B。

Result: 验证集BLEU 16.7，测试集BLEU 15.9、BLEURT 44.7；延迟优化方面，平均响应延迟从1.873秒降至1.354秒（降27.71%），P95延迟从2.919秒降至2.130秒（降27.03%）。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级微调与系统级工程优化，在保持可接受翻译质量的前提下显著降低了端到端延迟，验证了句子级手语翻译实时部署的可行性，且客户端无关设计支持多种前端设备。

Abstract: Most sign language understanding systems operate at the level of isolated signs, limiting their usefulness in natural communication. We study sentence-level sign language translation (SLT) with the primary goal of real-time deployment rather than proposing a new translation architecture. We fine-tune a SHuBERT-ByT5 translation stack on a uniformly sampled 9,872-example subset of How2Sign, selected because of compute and storage constraints, using QLoRA while keeping SHuBERT frozen. The model obtains a validation BLEU of 16.7 and, on the test split, BLEU 15.9 and BLEURT 44.7. The main contribution is a hardware-aware streaming system: a Raspberry Pi 4B reference client provides camera capture, local text display, and speech output, while compute-intensive perception and translation run on a CPU/GPU backend. The capture protocol remains client-agnostic, so the same backend can serve a browser, phone, or laptop. Chunked ingestion, bounded queues, parallelized perception, temporal reordering, and a sentence-boundary state machine reduce mean post-finalization response latency from 1.873 to 1.354 seconds (27.71%) and P95 latency from 2.919 to 2.130 seconds (27.03%) over the complete 9,872-example working subset.

</details>


### [93] [Task-Specific Multimodal Question Answering Agents via Confidence Calibration and Incremental Reasoning for QANTA 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09623)
*Nirjhar Das,Md. Al-Mamun Provath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务特定的双智能体架构，用于QANTA 2026多模态问答挑战，分别针对Tossup和Bonus问题采用不同的GPT-4模型和推理策略，在不依赖检索或集成的情况下，以0.402的总分获得排行榜第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多模态问答中资源效率约束下的两大挑战：Tossup问题需要在不完全信息下决定何时回答（不确定性决策），Bonus问题需要准确的答案选择与人类采纳。

Method: 提出任务特定的双智能体架构：Tossup智能体采用GPT-4o-mini级别模型，配备置信度校准回答机制和领域特定数值推理策略，减少孤立量化线索导致的过度自信预测；Bonus智能体采用GPT-4o级别模型，集成引导感知推理、结构化关系推理和多模态证据整合，提升精确答案选择能力。系统运行在纯托管环境中，不依赖检索管道或模型集成。

Result: 系统以0.402的总分获得排行榜最高分，其中Tossup得分0.238，Bonus效果得分0.164。

Conclusion: 轻量级、任务特定的推理策略能在资源受限的多模态问答基准上实现强劲性能，表明针对目标任务的精细化设计可替代复杂的检索或集成方案。

Abstract: We present our submission to the QANTA 2026 shared challenge at the ICML 2026 Workshop on Efficient Multimodal Question Answering (EMM-QA). Quanta evaluates multimodal quizbowl systems that answer pyramid-style questions from incrementally revealed text and accompanying images while operating under realistic efficiency constraints. The challenge consists of two distinct tasks: Tossup questions, which require deciding when to answer under uncertainty, and Bonus questions, which emphasize accurate answer selection and human adoption. To address these differing objectives, we develop a task-specific two-agent architecture. Our Tossup agent utilizes a GPT-4o-mini-class model (referred to as GPT-4.1-mini in the competition logs) with confidence-calibrated answering and a domain-specific numeric reasoning policy that reduces overconfident predictions from isolated quantitative clues. Our Bonus agent uses GPT-4o-class model (referred to as GPT-4.1) with leadin-aware reasoning, structured relational reasoning, and multimodal evidence integration to improve exact answer selection. Rather than relying on a retrieval pipeline or model ensembles, our approach emphasizes efficient reasoning policies and confidence calibration within a hosted-only environment. Our system achieved the highest overall leaderboard score of 0.402, including a Tossup score of 0.238 and a Bonus Effect score of 0.164. The results demonstrate that lightweight, task-specific reasoning strategies can provide strong performance on resource-constrained multimodal question answering benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Interval Certifications for Multilayered Perceptrons via Lattice Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08773)
*Merkouris Papamichail,Konstantinos Varsos,Giorgos Flouris,João Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将对抗鲁棒性问题归结为格点遍历问题，区分了"声音认证"（输入在区间内自由扰动不改变预测）和"完全认证"（超出区间预测必改变），开发了格遍历算子并通过"细化与验证"迭代方案保证最大/最小性，发现了优化问题中的非对称性（完全认证可多项式求解，声音认证为强难解问题），并在对称区间上提出了对数级算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为AI安全中的对抗鲁棒性建立严格的理论框架，并填补"完全认证"这一未被现有文献研究的概念空白。

Method: 将对抗鲁棒性归约为格点遍历问题，定义声音认证和完全认证两种区间概念；开发格遍历算子，采用"细化与验证"（refine & verify）迭代方案；利用形式化MLP验证器保证声音最大性和完全最小性；分析优化问题的计算复杂性。

Result: 完全认证的最小解可在多项式次oracle调用内求得；声音认证被证明为强难解问题；对称区间（ℓ∞球）上提出了对数级算法；通过ParallelepipedoNN系统进行了实证评估。

Conclusion: 对抗鲁棒性问题可归约为格遍历问题，且声音认证与完全认证之间在计算复杂度上存在显著非对称性，为AI安全提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: In this work we present a rigorous theoretical framework to a foundational problem of AI safety, namely adversarial robustness. In particular, we show that the adversarial robustness problem can be reduced to a lattice traversal problem. Each element of this lattice corresponds to an interval, i.e., an axis-aligned hyper-rectangle, containing an input point $\mathbf{x}$. Consider a multilayered perceptron classifier (MLP). An interval $I$ constitutes a sound certification if $\mathbf{x} \in I$ and $\mathbf{x}$ can be freely perturbed in $I$ without changing the MLP's prediction. Complementarily, an interval $I$ constitutes a complete certification if $\mathbf{x} \in I$ and when $\mathbf{x}$ moves outside of $I$ the MLP's prediction is guaranteed to change. While the sound certification problem corresponds to the well-studied adversarial robustness, complete certifications have not been examined in the literature. We develop lattice traversal operators, which we apply in a refine & verify iterative scheme. Using formal MLP verifiers, sound maximality and complete minimality are guaranteed. Moreover, we examine objective optimization problems. There we discover some interesting asymmetries. For complete certifications, the minimum solution is obtained in polynomial oracle calls. This does not hold for sound certifications, where we prove strong intractability results. Additionally, we examine optimization problems in symmetric intervals (i.e., $\ell_\infty$-spheres), where we provide logarithmic algorithms. Finally, we present an empirical evaluation, using the novel ParallelepipedoNN system.

</details>


### [95] [CogniConsole: Externalizing Inference-Time Control as a Formal Abstraction for Reliable LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08774)
*Vanessa Figueiredo,Wilter Franceschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CogniConsole架构，通过推理时控制（结构化接口与程序化协调）显著降低LLM输出方差和失败率，证明许多LLM故障源于控制不足而非能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战将LLM可靠性仅归因于模型能力的传统观点，论证推理时控制（任务框架与上下文选择）对可靠性的关键影响。

Method: 设计CogniConsole架构，将控制外部化为结构化接口，结合程序化协调与受限提示推理；通过489个可控性导向探针在多步交互环境中进行实验。

Result: 从非结构化到完全结构化的脚手架构建，系统性地降低了输出方差和失败率，且模型架构固定不变。

Conclusion: 许多故障模式（如上下文漂移、约束不一致）源于控制规格不足而非能力不足；推理时控制应作为一级抽象，为超越规模扩展的LLM系统设计与评估开辟新方向。

Abstract: Reliability in large language model (LLM) systems is typically framed as a function of model capability. We challenge this by demonstrating that reliability is significantly influenced by \emph{inference-time control} -- the computational layer governing task framing and context selection. We introduce \emph{CogniConsole}, an architectural instantiation that externalizes this control into a structured interface combining programmatic coordination with bounded prompt-based reasoning. Through \emph{controllability-oriented probes} ($N=489$) in a multi-step interactive environment, we show that increasing structural scaffolding -- from unstructured to fully scaffolded -- \textbf{systematically reduces output variance and failure rates under a fixed model architecture}. Our results indicate that many observed failure modes, such as context drift and inconsistent constraint adherence, arise from under-specified control rather than insufficient capability. This work provides an empirical basis for treating inference-time control as a first-class abstraction, opening new directions for designing and evaluating LLM systems beyond scaling alone.

</details>


### [96] [GATS: Graph-Augmented Tree Search with Layered World Models for Efficient Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08894)
*Maureese Williams,Dymitr Nowicki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GATS是一个结合UCB1树搜索与三层世界模型的规划框架，在推理阶段完全消除LLM调用，在合成任务上达到100%成功率（LATS为92%，ReAct为64%），在12个挑战性场景中保持100%成功率，且每任务零LLM调用、零方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体规划方法（如LATS、ReAct）在规划过程中严重依赖LLM推理，导致计算成本高昂且行为具有随机性，亟需一种更高效、确定性的规划方法。

Method: 提出GATS框架，核心组件包括：(1) 基于UCB1的系统化树搜索；(2) 三层世界模型——L1精确符号动作匹配、L2基于执行日志的统计学习、L3面向未知动作的LLM预测。规划阶段完全无需LLM调用。

Result: 合成任务：GATS 100%成功率 vs LATS 92% vs ReAct 64%。12个挑战场景：GATS 100% vs LATS 88.9% vs ReAct 23.9%。每任务LLM调用：GATS 0次 vs LATS 37次。GATS零方差，产生确定性规划。

Conclusion: 系统化的树搜索结合学习到的世界模型，在智能体规划任务上可以显著优于依赖LLM引导的探索方法，兼顾高效性与确定性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise in multi-step planning tasks, but existing approaches like LATS (Language Agent Tree Search) and ReAct rely heavily on LLM inference during planning, leading to high computational costs and stochastic behavior. We present \textbf{GATS} (Graph-Augmented Tree Search), a planning framework that combines systematic UCB1-based tree search with a layered world model to eliminate LLM calls during inference while achieving superior planning performance. Our three-layer world model integrates: (L1) exact symbolic action matching, (L2) statistics learned from execution logs, and (L3) LLM-based prediction for unknown actions. On synthetic planning tasks with branching paths and dead-ends, GATS achieves \textbf{100\% success rate} compared to 92 % for LATS and 64\% for ReAct. On a comprehensive stress test spanning 12 challenging scenarios -- including coding workflows, web navigation, and long-horizon tasks -- GATS maintains \textbf{100\% success} while LATS drops to 88.9 % and ReAct to 23.9%. GATS requires \textbf{zero LLM calls per task} during planning (vs. 37 per task for LATS) and produces deterministic plans with zero variance across runs. Our results demonstrate that systematic search with learned world models can substantially outperform LLM-guided exploration for agent planning.

</details>


### [97] [Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench: Testing the Limits of Agents on Long-Horizon Terminal Tasks with Dense Reward-Based Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08964)
*Zongxia Li,Zhongzhi Li,Yucheng Shi,Ruhan Wang,Junyao Yang,Zhichao Liu,Xiyang Wu,Anhao Li,Yue Yu,Ninghao Liu,Lichao Sun,Haotao Mi,LeoweiLiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench，一个包含46个长时域任务的终端基准测试，涵盖9个类别。任务被分解为细粒度子任务以提供密集中间奖励。评估15个前沿模型发现，即使最强模型在0.95部分奖励阈值下仅达15.2% pass@1，在1.0完美奖励阈值下为10.9%，平均通过率分别为4.3%和1.7%，表明现有智能体在长时域任务上仍有巨大改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端基准测试主要关注可在数分钟内完成的简单问题，仅通过最终结果评估，忽略了中间过程和部分解决方案，导致奖励信号稀疏且无法全面反映智能体能力。因此需要构建一个包含长时域、细粒度子任务分解的基准来更真实地评估智能体在复杂工作流上的表现。

Method: 构建包含46个长时域任务的基准，覆盖实验复现、软件工程、多模态分析、交互游戏、科学计算等9个类别。每个任务遵循Terminal-Bench风格设置，但进一步分解为细粒度分级子任务，以提供密集中间奖励和部分学分。任务通常需要数百个episode、数分钟到数小时的执行时间，强调长时域规划、长上下文管理和迭代调试能力。

Result: 评估15个前沿模型发现：智能体平均每任务消耗990万token，约231个episode，85.3分钟执行时间。最强模型在0.95部分奖励阈值下仅达15.2% pass@1，在1.0完美奖励阈值下为10.9%。所有模型在两个阈值下的平均通过率分别为4.3%和1.7%。

Conclusion: 现有智能体在长时域终端任务上表现有限，存在巨大改进空间。Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench比以往终端基准更具挑战性，可支持未来长时域终端智能体的研究进展。

Abstract: AI agents have become capable of autonomously completing short, well-specified tasks. However, existing terminal benchmarks largely focus on simple problems that finish within minutes and are evaluated only by their final outcome. This setup overlooks intermediate progress and partial solutions, yielding sparse reward signals and an incomplete picture of agent capability. We introduce Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench, a terminal benchmark of 46 long-horizon tasks spanning nine categories, including experiment reproduction, software engineering, multimodal analysis, interactive games, and scientific computing. Each task follows a Terminal-Bench-style setup with a reference solution or simulation engine, but is further decomposed into fine-grained graded subtasks. This design enables dense intermediate rewards and partial credit, allowing evaluation to capture not only whether an agent reaches the final goal, but also how far it progresses on open-ended workflows. Tasks in Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench typically require hundreds of episodes and minutes to hours of execution, stressing long-horizon planning, long-context management, and iterative debugging rather than one-shot problem solving. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find that agents consume on average 9.9M tokens per task, with roughly 231 episodes and 85.3 minutes of execution time per run, making Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench more demanding than prior terminal-based benchmarks. Even the strongest tested model achieves 15.2% pass@1 at a partial-reward threshold of 0.95 and 10.9% at a perfect-reward threshold of 1.0, while the mean pass rate across models is 4.3% and 1.7% under the two thresholds, respectively. These results reveal headroom for improvement. We further analyze failure modes and error patterns, and release Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench to support future progress on long-horizon terminal agents.

</details>


### [98] [A Formalization of the Mean-Field Derivation of the Vlasov Equation: AI-Assisted Lean Formalization as a Strategy Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08986)
*Joseph K. Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以形式化游戏的方式，由数学家指导AI系统将LaTeX文档转化为Lean 4证明助理中的完整、公理干净的形式化代码。以非线性Vlasov方程（通过Dobrushin平均场路径）的适定性为案例，完成了存在性、唯一性、稳定性估计、平均场极限及短时间叠加原理的形式化证明。其中约六分之一的代码（最优传输相关）可独立提取为与Mathlib兼容的通用数学层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索由数学家指导、AI执行的形式化证明新范式，弥合LaTeX论文与机器可验证证明之间的鸿沟，并衡量形式化过程中产生的可复用数学基础设施。

Method: 将形式化过程建模为游戏：人类数学家负责范围界定、分解策略和填补库缺口，AI代理负责具体证明编写。以Dobrushin平均场路径下的非线性Vlasov方程适定性为案例，最终通过Lean 4编译并验证公理独立性。

Result: 完整形式化了Vlasov方程的存在性、唯一性、稳定性估计、平均场极限和短时间叠加原理；299个声明中49个（约六分之一）可提取为自包含的通用数学层，仅依赖Mathlib核心库；头版定理约一周完成，完整开发约一个月。

Conclusion: 人机协作的形式化游戏范式可行且高效，最优传输理论的形式化成果可反哺数学库；方法论框架独立于具体工具，具有持久适用性；但定量观察仅基于单次实验，不视为普适规律。

Abstract: We formalize a research result in the Lean 4 proof assistant by having a mathematician direct an AI system, and frame the activity as a formalization game. The objective is to turn a LaTeX document into Lean. The game is won when the development compiles, contains no sorry, and a machine check shows the target theorems rest on Lean's foundational axioms alone. Reuse is a second check, by a definition we introduce: whether the development yields a self-contained layer of general mathematics the wider library could absorb. The case study is a complete, axiom-clean formalization of well-posedness for the nonlinear Vlasov equation via Dobrushin's mean-field route -- existence, uniqueness, the stability estimate and mean-field limit, and a short-window superposition principle (weak solutions are Lagrangian). The human's role was to direct, not to write proofs: to scope the definitions, steer the decompositions, and triage the library's gaps; the AI agent executed. The formalization certifies the proof of each statement as written; whether the written statement is the intended theorem stays the mathematician's judgment. The optimal-transport machinery that fell out of the build (in particular, properties of the Wasserstein-1 metric and the Kantorovich-Rubinstein duality theorem) separates into a self-contained layer that compiles against Mathlib alone: about a sixth of the development (49 of 299 declarations), behind a 22-declaration interface with no reverse dependency. The headline theorems ran in about a week, the full development in about a month. We report the quantitative claims as observations of one game, not as general laws. The game's rules name no particular system, so the methodological framing is meant to outlast the tools of any one run.

</details>


### [99] [ARCANA: A Reflective Multi-Agent Program Synthesis Framework for ARC-AGI-2 Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09059)
*Kunbo Zhang,Lei Fu,Zeyu Wang,Zijing Liu,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARCANA是一个协作式多智能体框架，通过迭代感知、假设生成、符号执行和反思改进四个阶段，在严格时间和硬件约束下解决ARC AGI 2抽象转换任务。智能体通过可微分黑板通信，由元控制器调度，结合结构化程序搜索与自适应多轮修正，提升推理效率和解决方案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格的时间和硬件约束下，解决ARC AGI 2这类抽象视觉推理任务面临巨大挑战，现有方法要么依赖大规模计算资源，要么缺乏结构化推理能力，难以高效生成高质量解决方案。

Method: 采用多智能体协作框架，包含四个核心模块：(1)感知接地智能体，从原始网格构建以对象为中心的场景图；(2)潜在程序策略，生成多样化的领域特定语言(DSL)程序；(3)符号执行器，在演示样本上验证候选程序；(4)反思智能体，综合失败驱动的反馈用于下一轮迭代。智能体通过可微分黑板共享信息，由元控制器调度执行。

Result: 在抽象转换任务上，该方法相比基线显著提升了推理效率和解决方案质量，能够在严格约束下有效解决ARC AGI任务。

Conclusion: 将结构化程序搜索与自适应多轮修正相结合的多智能体协作范式，是解决受限条件下抽象推理任务的有效路径，元控制器调度和反思反馈机制是提升性能的关键组件。

Abstract: We present ARCANA, a collaborative multi agent framework for solving ARC AGI 2 tasks under strict test time and hardware constraints. ARCANA decomposes each task into iterative perception, hypothesis generation, symbolic execution, and reflective refinement. A perceptual grounding agent builds object centric scene graphs from raw grids, a latent program policy proposes diverse DSL programs, a symbolic executor verifies candidates on demonstrations, and a reflective agent synthesizes failure driven feedback for the next turn. These agents communicate through a shared differentiable blackboard and are scheduled by a learned meta controller. The design combines structured program search with adaptive multi turn correction, improving reasoning efficiency and solution quality on challenging abstract transformation tasks.

</details>


### [100] [Neuro-Agentic Control: A Deep Learning-based LLM-Powered Agentic AI Framework for Controlling Security Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09076)
*Saroj Gopali,Bipin Chhetri,Deepika Giri,Sima Siami-Namini,Akbar Siami Namin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经-代理控制框架，将基于LLM的规划器（如Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite）与预训练时间序列基础模型（TimesFM）耦合，通过“反事实物理注入”机制在驱动前模拟LLM提议干预的后果，从而拒绝幻觉或不安全动作。在SWaT工业数据集上，该框架优于LSTM和TCN基线，实现了零物理无效动作执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对工业物联网环境中传统基于规则的监控方法在应对日益频繁的网络攻击时的局限性，以及LLM虽具备语义推理能力但存在幻觉问题、无法直接用于闭环控制的安全隐患，作者提出一种将LLM的推理能力与基础模型的物理约束相结合的新型架构。

Method: 提出神经-代理控制框架：1）LLM规划器（Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite）生成干预方案；2）预训练时间序列基础模型（TimesFM）作为数值潜空间的物理仿真器；3）“反事实物理注入”机制在驱动前模拟LLM提议动作的物理后果，过滤幻觉或不安全动作；4）形成神经-代理循环，仅执行通过物理验证的动作。

Result: 在SWaT数据集随机攻击场景下，该框架相比LSTM（26.7%）和TCN（13.3%）基线，将阈值以下的安全违规阻止率提升至33.3%，且执行了零物理无效（幻觉）动作。

Conclusion: 将基础模型作为确定性“哨兵”来守护代理AI的方法在关键基础设施中有效可行，能够在不牺牲LLM推理能力的前提下消除其安全风险，为工业自主防御提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cyberattacks on operational technology are increasingly causing costly downtime and physical damage, exposing the limitations of traditional rule-based monitoring in industrial IoT environments. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have strong semantic reasoning abilities to assist in decision support, their hallucinatory nature presents unacceptable safety liabilities for closed-loop control. This paper introduces a neuro-agentic control framework, a novel architecture that couples an LLM-based planner (i.e., such as Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite) with a pre-trained Time-Series Foundation Model (TimesFM), to achieve physics-grounded autonomous defense. The paper introduces a ``Counterfactual Physics Injection'' mechanism that simulates the impact of LLM-proposed interventions within the numerical latent space of the foundation model before actuation, while allowing the system to reject hallucinatory or unsafe actions. Evaluated on an industrial dataset (e.g., the Secure Water Treatment (SWaT)) in the context of stochastic attack scenarios, the framework exhibited better performance compared to LSTM and TCN baselines. The Neuro-Agentic Loop prevented five breaches (33.3%) below the threshold versus LSTM (26.7%) and TCN (13.3%), with zero physically invalid (hallucinated) actions executed. These results demonstrate the efficacy of using foundation models as deterministic ``Sentinels'' to safeguard agentic AI in critical infrastructure.

</details>


### [101] [L-MAD: A Systematic Evaluation of Multi-Agent Debate Structures in Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09099)
*Tan-Minh Nguyen,Hoang-Trung Nguyen,Huu-Dong Nguyen,Dinh-Truong Do,Thi-Hai-Yen Vuong,Le-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出法律多智能体辩论框架（L-MAD），通过赋予不同专家角色和系统评估辩论结构与聚合方法，在法律文本蕴含任务中提升性能达8%，同时发现增加智能体数量可降低不一致性并提高准确性，但延长讨论轮次会导致过度 deliberation 漂移——智能体相互强化错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论框架在通用推理中已展现潜力，但在高度结构化、知识密集型的法律领域中的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Legal Multi-Agent Debate (L-MAD)框架，为多个智能体分配不同的专家角色，系统评估不同辩论结构和聚合方法在法律文本蕴含任务上的表现。

Result: 相比强单智能体基线提升高达8%；增加智能体数量可降低不一致性并提高准确性；延长讨论轮次会导致"过度 deliberation 漂移"，即智能体相互强化错误。

Conclusion: 在高风险法律推理场景中部署协作式多智能体系统存在明确的实践边界与安全边际，揭示了辩论轮次并非越多越好的关键限制。

Abstract: While multi-agent debate (MAD) frameworks have shown significant potential in general reasoning, their effectiveness in highly structured, knowledge-heavy legal domains remains under-explored. In this work, we introduce the Legal Multi-Agent Debate (L-MAD) framework to systematically evaluate different debate structures and aggregation methods within Legal Textual Entailment. By assigning distinct expert personas to multiple agents, L-MAD improves upon strong single-agent baselines by up to 8\%. Furthermore, analyzing how debate scales reveals a clear trade-off: increasing the agent population reduces inconsistency and improves accuracy, whereas extending discussion rounds induces a detrimental \textit{over-deliberation drift} where agents reinforce each other's mistakes. Ultimately, our findings outline the practical boundaries and safety margins of deploying collaborative multi-agent systems in high-stakes legal reasoning environments.

</details>


### [102] [MedRealMM: A Real-World Multimodal Benchmark for Chinese Online Medical Consultation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09142)
*Runhan Shi,Quan Zhou,Yuqian Xu,Shuai Yang,Xin Wu,Zitong Zhou,Hui Liu,Bin Cha,Zheming Wang,Liya Li,Wei Wei,Haoyuan Hu,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedRealMM是一个基于真实医患交互数据构建的大规模多模态在线医疗咨询基准，包含5,620个真实病例，覆盖64个科室。通过MCCP框架提取临床关键时刻并转化为下一响应生成任务，使用医生制定的案例特定评分标准进行评估。评估19个LLM后发现：图像信息对临床性能至关重要，当前最先进模型虽在某些正面标准上可达到或超过医生水平，但触发了更多负面标准，安全敏感性错误避免仍是核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM医疗咨询基准与真实临床实践严重脱节：多使用合成对话或患者模拟器、忽略患者上传的医学影像、采用多选题或词汇重叠指标评估开放式临床回复，无法真实反映临床质量。

Method: 从全国互联网医院收集脱敏的真实医患对话，提出MCCP（多模态临床挑战点）提取框架，在真实咨询轨迹中识别临床关键时刻，将其转化为标准化下一响应生成任务，同时保留前序文本-图像上下文。每个实例由医生制定案例特定评分标准，奖励临床合理行为，惩罚不安全、无依据或矛盾回复。

Result: 评估19个通用和医学专用LLM（含纯文本和多模态系统），发现：1）图像信息对可靠临床性能至关重要；2）当前前沿模型整体仍低于在线医生水平；3）部分前沿模型在满足正面临床标准上可与医生相当甚至超越，但触发更多负面标准，表明安全敏感性错误避免是核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: MedRealMM提供了一个真实的、可复现的基准，用于评估真实世界在线医疗咨询中的多模态医学推理能力。数据集将在Hugging Face公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in online medical consultation, yet existing benchmarks remain poorly aligned with real clinical practice. Many rely on synthetic conversations or patient simulators, omit patient-uploaded medical images, or evaluate open-ended clinical responses using multiple-choice or lexical-overlap metrics that poorly reflect clinical quality. We introduce \textbf{MedRealMM}, a large-scale benchmark for multimodal online medical consultation built from de-identified patient-doctor interactions collected from a nationwide Chinese internet hospital. MedRealMM uses a Multimodal Clinical Challenge Point (MCCP) extraction framework to identify clinically demanding moments in authentic consultation trajectories and converts each into a standardized next-response generation task while preserving the preceding text-image context. Each instance is paired with a case-specific rubric refined by physicians that rewards clinically desirable behaviors and penalizes unsafe, unsupported, or contradictory responses. The current release contains 5,620 real-world multimodal cases spanning 64 clinical departments. We evaluate 19 general-purpose and medical-specialized LLMs, including text-only and multimodal systems. Our results show that image information is critical for reliable clinical performance and that current frontier models remain below the online physician response. Although some frontier models satisfy as many or more positive clinical criteria than physicians, they trigger more negative criteria, indicating that safety-sensitive error avoidance remains a central bottleneck. MedRealMM offers a realistic and reproducible benchmark for evaluating multimodal medical reasoning in real-world online consultation. The dataset will be publicly available on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jdh-algo/MedRealMM.

</details>


### [103] [KV-PRM: Efficient Process Reward Modeling via KV-Cache Transfer for Multi-Agent Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09153)
*Peng Kuang,Haibo Jin,Xiaoyu Han,Yanli Wang,Xiaopeng Yuan,Ye Yu,Kaidi Xu,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KV-PRM 通过直接利用 LLM 生成阶段产生的 KV 缓存而非重新编码文本，将过程奖励模型的评分成本从 O(L²) 降至 O(L)，在保持或超越文本 PRM 性能的同时，实现了最高 5000 倍 FLOPs 减少、37 倍延迟降低和 34 倍内存压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本 PRM 在多智能体长轨迹场景下需要从头重新编码整个轨迹文本，评分成本随序列长度 L 呈二次增长 O(L²)，造成严重的计算瓶颈，限制了其在长上下文场景中的应用。

Method: 提出 KV-PRM，直接利用 LLM 生成阶段自然产生的 KV 缓存，通过处理单个"验证 token"与已有 KV 缓存的交互来完成奖励建模，避免了文本重新编码；并从理论上证明了 KV 缓存包含比文本严格更多的信息容量。

Result: 在 MATH、GSM8K 和 AIME 基准上，KV-PRM 在 Beam Search、MCTS、Weighted Voting 等 TTS 方法下匹配或严格超越文本 PRM，同时实现评分 FLOPs 最高减少 5000 倍、延迟降低 37 倍、每序列内存占用减少 34 倍。

Conclusion: KV-PRM 有效解决了长上下文场景下 PRM 的计算效率问题，为多智能体系统的测试时扩展提供了可行的加速方案。

Abstract: Process Reward Models (PRMs) have been proven to be highly effective in guiding test-time scaling (TTS) methods, which significantly boost the capabilities of LLM-based multi-agent systems. However, existing PRMs are text-based: they re-encode the entire trajectory text from scratch. In long multi-agent rollouts, the scoring cost, growing quadratically with respect to sequence length L, creates a severe computational bottleneck, severely limiting PRMs' application in long-context scenarios. To resolve this, we introduce KV-PRM, a highly efficient process reward model that eliminates the heavy text re-encoding by directly reading the KV cache produced naturally during the LLM's generation phase. By processing a single "verify token" against the pre-existing KV cache, KV-PRM reduces the scoring cost from O(L^2) to O(L). We formally prove that the KV cache contains strictly greater information capacity than text, and is more efficient for downstream reward modeling. Empirically, across the MATH, GSM8K, and AIME benchmarks, KV-PRM matches or strictly outperforms text-PRMs under various TTS methods such as Beam Search, MCTS, and Weighted Voting, with up to a 5,000x reduction in scoring FLOPs, a 37x reduction in latency, and a 34x reduction in per-sequence memory footprint compared to text-based PRMs.

</details>


### [104] [Scoped Verification for Reliable Long-Horizon Agentic Context Evolution under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09175)
*Dan C. Hsu,Luke Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRACE方法，将LLM智能体持久化系统指令维护为类型化语义图，通过局部图邻域验证更新并重构为文本增量，在电信智能体基准测试中，将严格可靠性从0.091提升至0.673±0.136，显著优于文本基线（0.191±0.051）和Gemini 3.1 Pro零样本参考值（0.242）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的LLM智能体依赖智能体上下文（模型外部文本控制内容），其可变部分为持久化系统指令。随着运行经验积累，扁平文本维护导致指令不断累积和交互，使验证越来越困难，因此需要一种结构化的上下文演化方法来解决可验证性问题。

Method: GRACE将持久化指令组件维护为类型化语义图，在修改节点的局部类型化邻域内验证提议的更新，接受后的图更新被重构为文本指令检查点的增量编辑，用于部署。

Result: 在固定电信智能体框架下，经过受控分布偏移协议，五次独立重复实验中，GRACE将pass^3指标从Gemini 2.5 Flash零样本的0.091提升至最终检查点的0.673±0.136，超过Gemini 3.1 Pro零样本参考值0.242；扁平文本基线HCE仅达到0.191±0.051。

Conclusion: 可靠的长时域上下文演化需要两个条件：使验证局部化的结构基底，以及保持累积指令内容可用性的整合机制。

Abstract: Deployed LLM agents rely on agentic context, the model-external textual control content assembled by an operational harness. In this work, the mutable component of that context is a persistent system-level instruction that is updated from operational experience while the model, tools, and harness remain fixed. Over long evolution horizons, flat-text maintenance makes verification increasingly difficult as accumulated instructions grow and interact. We propose Graph-Regularized Agentic Context Evolution (GRACE), which maintains the persistent instruction component as a typed semantic graph and validates proposed updates within the local typed neighborhoods of modified nodes. Accepted graph updates are reconstructed as incremental edits to the textual instruction checkpoint used at deployment. We evaluate GRACE within a fixed telecom agent harness derived from $τ^2$-bench under a controlled distribution-shift protocol. Across five independent replications, GRACE improves strict reliability, measured by pass^3, from the Gemini 2.5 Flash zero-shot value of 0.091 to 0.673$\pm$0.136 at the final checkpoint. This exceeds a Gemini 3.1 Pro zero-shot reference of 0.242 on the same held-out set, while the flat-text HCE baseline finishes at 0.191$\pm$0.051. These results identify two requirements for reliable long-horizon context evolution, a structural substrate that makes verification local and a consolidation mechanism that keeps accumulated instruction content usable.

</details>


### [105] [Toward Auditable AI Scientists: A Hypothesis Evolution Protocol for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09195)
*Izumi Takahara,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出假设演化协议（HEP），一种让LLM智能体在科学发现中进行显式、可审计的假设生成、测试、评估和信念更新的框架，实验证明其在材料科学研究任务中优于传统规划式智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在科学发现过程中，假设、测试和信念更新都隐藏在非结构化日志中，缺乏可审计机制，使得智能体自身和人类研究者都无法审查这一过程。

Method: 提出假设演化协议（HEP），一种智能体外挂框架，将假设生成、评估和演化作为显式、可审计的操作进行管理，实现假设-测试-证据-信念的完整循环。

Result: 在材料科学研究任务上，配备HEP的智能体能够执行规划式智能体所缺乏的完整假设-测试-证据-信念循环，能跨研究问题进行泛化，并且随着基础LLM能力增强能更充分地利用该协议。

Conclusion: 该工作向可审计的AI科学家迈出了关键一步，使得科学推理过程可以被检查、验证和在此基础上继续构建。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to play a central role in AI-driven scientific discovery. Equipped with broad knowledge, flexible reasoning, and tool use, they have the potential to autonomously explore and solve scientific problems by repeatedly proposing hypotheses, testing them, and revising their beliefs in the light of the evidence. In current agents, however, these hypotheses, tests, and belief updates are buried in unstructured logs, and no mechanism lets the agent or the human researcher audit that process. Here we propose the Hypothesis Evolution Protocol (HEP), an agent harness that provides hypothesis generation, evaluation, and evolution as explicit, auditable operations. On materials-science research tasks, a HEP-equipped agent operates the hypothesis--test--evidence--belief cycle that planning-style agents lack, generalizes across research questions, and exploits the protocol more fully as the base LLM becomes more capable. These results mark a step toward auditable AI scientists, whose scientific reasoning can be inspected, verified, and built upon.

</details>


### [106] [OpenProver: Agentic and Interactive Theorem Proving with Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09217)
*Matěj Kripner,Milan Straka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenProver是一个开源的、基于LLM的自动定理证明系统，采用Planner-Worker-Verifier架构，支持Lean 4形式化验证，提供交互式终端界面，并在ProofNet上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动定理证明系统缺乏开源实现和可复现评估，同时希望探索人类-AI协同在定理证明中的潜力。

Method: 采用Planner-Worker-Verifier架构：Planner代理维护紧凑的Whiteboard草稿板和无限的Repository存储中间发现，将数学工作分解为并行的Worker。系统支持自动形式化验证和交互式人类引导的证明搜索。

Result: 在ProofNet基准上进行了定量消融实验评估，并与简单基线进行了比较。

Conclusion: OpenProver作为完全开源的系统，通过自动形式化验证实现了可复现评估，交互式模式展示了人机协同在定理证明中的价值。

Abstract: In this system paper, we present OpenProver, an open-source system for LLM-driven automated theorem proving (ATP) with integrated Lean 4 formal verification. OpenProver integrates a Planner-Worker-Verifier architecture inspired by recent ATP agentic systems such as Aletheia. A Planner agent maintains a compact Whiteboard scratchpad and an unbounded Repository of intermediate findings, and decomposes mathematical work into parallel Workers.
  OpenProver is fully open-source, offers reproducible evaluation through automatic formal verification of generated proofs, and provides an interactive terminal interface for human-guided proof search. In interactive mode, OpenProver allows the human operator to monitor and steer the proof search process, motivated by the established human-AI synergy in interactive code generation.
  To showcase the potential for quantitative ablation experiments enabled by automatic formal verification, we evaluate OpenProver on ProofNet and compare it with a simple baseline. OpenProver is publicly available at https://github.com/kripner/OpenProver.

</details>


### [107] [LongMedBench: Benchmarking Medical Agents for Long-Horizon Clinical Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09322)
*Yanzhen Chen,Zihan Xu,Xiaocheng Zhang,Zhiting Fan,Weiqi Zhai,Hongxia Xu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LongMedBench是一个基于真实世界EHR数据的长期临床决策基准，包含335名患者、平均19.72次住院和44.91个医疗事件，通过三个评估套件（事实QA、时间推理、长期决策）测试LLM医疗智能体的长期交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM医疗智能体评估多集中于短上下文知识问答和工具使用，但现实医疗是纵向的，医生需要跨多次就诊、检查和治疗方案聚合证据，因此需要长期交互能力的评估基准。

Method: 通过可复现的pipeline整合MIMIC-IV入院记录和临床笔记，构建时间序列事件流和长上下文记忆数据集，实现智能体与临床环境的多会话长期交互，并提出包含事实QA、时间推理和长期决策三套件的评估分类体系。

Result: 现有LLM能有效利用显式时间戳，但在隐式时间推理上存在困难；RAG和智能体记忆系统可提升信息检索任务性能，但决策任务性能高度依赖模型的即时上下文。

Conclusion: LongMedBench为评估LLM在长期临床决策中的能力提供了标准化基准，揭示了当前模型在隐式时间推理和长期决策方面的局限性，为未来医疗AI研究指明了方向。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce LongMedBench, a real-world EHR-based benchmark for long-horizon clinical decision-making. Prior evaluations of LLM-based medical agents have largely emphasized short-context knowledge QA and tool use. However, real-world medical care is inherently longitudinal, and clinicians must aggregate evidence across repeated visits, tests, and evolving treatments. Therefore, long-horizon interaction is essential for realistic assessment. LongMedBench is constructed via a reproducible pipeline that integrates MIMIC-IV admission records and clinical notes into time-series event streams and long-context memory datasets, enabling long-horizon, multi-session interactions between agents and a clinical environment. It comprises 335 patients, with 19.72 inpatient visits per patient on average and 44.91 medical events per visit. Guided by the long-horizon decision process, we propose an evaluation taxonomy with three suites: fact-based QA, temporal reasoning, and long-horizon decision-making. This taxonomy measures how agents understand and leverage historical patient information over extended horizons. Our experiments show that while recent LLMs can make good use of explicit timestamps, they have challenges in implicit time inference; The RAG and agent memory system can improve the performance of information retrieval tasks, but the performance of decision-making tasks is highly dependent on the model's immediate context.

</details>


### [108] [Communication-Efficient Digital-Twin Coordination for Heterogeneous LLM Embodied Agents over Computing Power Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09330)
*Nuocheng Yang,Sihua Wang,Zihan Chen,Tony Q. S. Quek,Changchuan Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LDT-Coord框架，利用轻量级数字孪生解耦异构LLM智能体团队的协作与自然语言推理能力，通过结构化约束报告和规则化协调器解决冲突，并采用C-POMDP和PPO-Lagrangian优化通信开销，在保持任务成功率的同时将通信开销降低70倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构LLM智能体协调框架依赖多轮自然语言对话，面临通信开销随团队规模快速增长、协调质量受限于LLM异构能力、以及迭代协商导致动作延迟等三个耦合挑战。

Method: 提出LDT-Coord框架：1) 每个智能体独立选择动作并报告动作决策及结构化时间约束给数字孪生服务器，将协调性能与自然语言推理能力解耦；2) 数字孪生执行无需训练的规则化协调算法解决跨智能体冲突并返回指令；3) 将智能体报告控制建模为约束部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(C-POMDP)，使用PPO-Lagrangian算法求解以降低通信开销。

Result: 仿真结果表明，LDT-Coord在保持与传统协调方法相当的任务成功率的同时，将通信开销降低70倍以上，并在LLM异构性下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于轻量级数字孪生的结构化协调框架能有效解耦异构LLM智能体协作中的通信、能力异质性和延迟问题，实现了高效鲁棒的团队协调。
