Abstract: Embodied agent teams powered by heterogeneous large language models (LLMs) are being widely deployed in physical artificial intelligence such as smart factories, warehouses, and service robotics. To enable collaboration among such an agent team, efficient coordination mechanisms that operate reliably under limited network resources are required. However, existing heterogeneous LLM-agent coordination frameworks that rely on multi-round natural-language-based conversations introduce three coupled challenges. First, inter-agent dialogue incurs communication overhead that grows rapidly with team size. Second, the quality of coordination is constrained by the heterogeneous capabilities of the agent team's LLMs. Third, agents may suffer from action delays due to iterative negotiation. To address these challenges, we propose LDT-Coord, a networked coordination framework built upon a lightweight digital twin (DT). Specifically, each agent independently selects its intended action and reports both the action decision and a structured temporal constraint over shared resources to the DT server, thereby decoupling coordination performance from natural-language reasoning ability. Then, DT executes a training-free, rule-based orchestrator algorithm to resolve cross-agent conflicts and returns coordination instructions to prevent such conflicts. To further reduce communication overhead, we formulate agent reporting control as a constrained partially observable Markov decision process (C-POMDP) and solve it with the PPO-Lagrangian algorithm. Simulation results show that LDT-Coord achieves a task success rate comparable to conventional coordination methods while reducing communication overhead by more than 70x and maintaining robustness under LLM heterogeneity.

</details>


### [109] [Fictional Worldbuilding: Multi-Agent LLM Collaboration with Hierarchical Context Compression and Iterative Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09403)
*Jingbo Chen,He Wang,Wei Yuan,Yuqiao Lai,Zhenyan Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoWorldBuilder是一个多智能体协作系统，通过结构化概念网络、DAG混合批调度器、四层上下文压缩（~90% token缩减）、迭代审核系统（提案通过率从42%提升至85%+）和技能驱动架构，解决大语言模型在虚构世界构建中的上下文爆炸、创意多样性与一致性矛盾、缺乏自动化质量保证三大挑战。在20项任务中实现95%成功率，每世界生成56-103个自洽概念，零冲突交付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自动化虚构世界构建中面临三个关键挑战：1) 上下文爆炸（随构建过程线性增长）；2) 创意多样性与内容一致性之间的矛盾；3) 缺乏自动化质量保证机制。

Method: 提出AutoWorldBuilder系统，包含五个集成组件：1) 带冲突检测的结构化概念网络；2) 基于DAG的混合批调度器（按语义局部性分组任务）；3) 四层上下文压缩机制（实现约90% token缩减）；4) 迭代审核系统（含专门Auditor智能体，将提案通过率从42%提升至85%以上）；5) 支持零代码扩展的技能驱动智能体架构（差异化温度配置）。

Result: 在20个多样化世界构建任务中，使用GPT-OSS 120B和DeepSeek v3.2作为LLM后端，实现95.0%成功率。系统在18-31分钟内为每个世界生成56-103个自洽概念，且零冲突交付。

Conclusion: 验证的架构模式（包括层即预算压缩、语义局部性调度、生成与审核分离）可迁移至更广泛的知识密集型多智能体LLM应用场景。

Abstract: Worldbuilding, the construction of coherent fictional worlds, is a foundational task in game design and literary creation. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new possibilities for automated content generation, but their application to worldbuilding faces three challenges: context explosion that grows linearly with the building process, the tension between creative diversity and content consistency, and the absence of automated quality assurance. This paper presents AutoWorldBuilder, a multi-agent collaborative system that addresses these challenges through five integrated components: a structured concept network with conflict detection; a DAG-based hybrid batch scheduler that groups tasks by semantic locality; a four-layer context compression mechanism achieving approximately 90% token reduction; an iterative review system with specialized Auditor agents that improves proposal pass rates from 42% to over 85%; and a skill-driven agent architecture supporting zero-code extension with differentiated temperature configuration. Two experiments across 20 diverse worldbuilding tasks, using GPT-OSS 120B and DeepSeek v3.2 as LLM backends, demonstrate a 95.0% success rate. The system generated 56-103 self-consistent concepts per world in 18-31 minutes with zero-conflict delivery. The architectural patterns validated here, including layer-as-budget compression, semantic-locality scheduling, and separation of generation and review, transfer to the broader class of knowledge-intensive, multi-agent LLM applications.

</details>


### [110] [How Does Bayesian Causal Discovery Fail? Characterising Structural Consequences in Linear Gaussian Networks under Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09449)
*Debargha Ghosh,Silja Renooij,Anna Kononova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了线性高斯因果模型中潜在混淆变量对贝叶斯因果发现后验分布的影响，推导出促使模型错误添加虚假边的相关阈值，并发现该阈值随样本量增大而降低，同时识别出两种后验失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯因果发现研究虽承认潜在混淆会破坏可识别性，但缺乏对后验分布如何具体响应的理论刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，定量分析混淆变量对DAG后验推断的影响机制。

Method: 针对线性高斯因果模型，聚焦于恰好两个观测变量之间存在加性潜在混淆的场景。通过理论推导得到评分函数倾向于在混淆变量间引入虚假边的临界相关阈值，并分析该阈值随样本量的变化规律，进一步根据混淆变量周围的局部结构区分两种后验失效模式。

Result: 推导出临界相关阈值，发现该阈值随样本量增加而降低（更多数据反而更容易导致虚假边被偏好）；识别出两种后验失效模式，并在多种图结构上通过精确后验计算验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 潜在混淆在贝叶斯因果发现中会导致系统性的后验偏差，其影响随数据量增大而加剧，且不同的局部图结构会引发不同的失效模式。这提醒研究者在使用贝叶斯方法进行因果发现时需警惕潜在混淆带来的误导性置信度。

Abstract: Bayesian causal discovery is widely used for its ability to quantify epistemic uncertainty over directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) through posterior inference. However, its behaviour under latent confounding remains poorly understood, as existing work typically notes that confounding breaks identifiability without characterising how the posterior distribution over DAGs responds. In this work, we analyse posterior behaviour under latent confounding in linear Gaussian causal models, focusing on additive latent confounding between exactly two observed variables. We derive a critical correlation threshold above which the score function favours graphs with a spurious edge between the confounded variables, and show that this threshold decreases with sample size -- more data lowers the correlation required for the spurious edge to be favoured. Beyond this threshold, we characterize two distinct posterior failure regimes determined by the local structure around the confounded variables. Our findings are supported by exact posterior computations on multiple graph structures, demonstrating both the predicted failure regimes.

</details>


### [111] [ProofCouncil: An LLM Agent for Solving Open Mathematical Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09474)
*Johannes Schmitt,Tim Gehrunger,Jasper Dekoninck,Gergely Bérczi,Uri Kreitner,Liam Price,David Holmes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProofCouncil是一个基于作者-评论家架构的数学智能体，在FirstProof挑战中解决了10个开放数学问题中的6个（最高分），并在30个开放问题中取得5个完全正确、2个有希望、8个部分进展的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在解决数学开放问题方面展现出潜力，但通过适配真实数学实践的智能体工作流可以进一步提升其性能。

Method: 采用作者-评论家(author-critic)架构设计数学智能体，并开发了用于构建该智能体的开源库。

Result: 在FirstProof挑战的10个问题中，6个被评审判定为最多只需小幅修改即可正确，表现最佳；在30个开放问题中，21个获得人类反馈，其中5个完全正确，2个有希望，8个包含有用的部分进展。

Conclusion: ProofCouncil展示了LLM智能体在解决实际数学开放问题上的有效性，相关工作已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in solving open problems in mathematics. However, their performance can be further improved through agentic workflows tailored to real-world mathematical practice. To this end, we introduce ProofCouncil, a mathematical agent that is designed to tackle open problems using an author-critic architecture. ProofCouncil served as a submission to the second batch of FirstProof, a challenge consisting of 10 real-world mathematical problems that agents must solve autonomously. Its submissions for 6 of the 10 problems were judged by the referees to be correct up to at most minor revisions, showing the best performance among participating teams. We also evaluate ProofCouncil on 30 open problems collected from mathematical researchers. Among the 21 solutions that received human feedback, 5 were judged completely correct, 2 more were judged promising pending final verification, and a further 8 contained useful partial progress. In this short paper, we describe the development of ProofCouncil and the agent-building library used to create it, which we release as open source to the community.

</details>


### [112] [Ceci n'est pas une pipe: AI systems as semantic abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09489)
*Jade Alglave,Patrick Cousot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个语义框架来区分AI系统输出中的知识、引用和可用信息，精确定义外推、无依据断言、来源与知识不匹配等常见失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统的输出看似描述事实，实则是工程化的表征。当前缺乏精确语义工具来检查这些表征的正确性，需要区分"被领域知识证成的"、"引用来源所说的"和"系统当前可用的"三者。

Method: 建立一个语义框架，对AI系统的输出进行分层：证成知识（justified by domain knowledge）、引用来源（what reference sources say）、系统可用信息（what the system can currently use），并基于此给出各类失败模式的精确语义定义。

Result: 给出了外推（extrapolation）、被反驳或无依据的断言、来源与知识不匹配、过时或被反驳的来源、添加假设、无依据使用等常见AI系统故障的精确语义定义。

Conclusion: 该框架为规范和检查AI系统提供了有用的术语表，使系统输出、引用、工具调用和改变世界的行动必须由可靠主张和明确权威而非表面流畅性来证成。

Abstract: An AI system's output is not the fact or world state it appears to describe, but rather an engineered representation. We propose a semantic framework to describe AI systems, to be able to examine the correctness of such representations. To do so, we distinguish what is justified by accepted domain knowledge, what reference sources say, and what the system can currently use. This allows us to give precise definitions to common failures: extrapolation, refuted or unsupported assertion, sources versus knowledge mismatch, stale or refuted source, added hypotheses, unsupported use... We hope our framework gives a useful vocabulary for specifying and checking AI systems whose outputs, citations, tool calls, and world-changing actions must be justified by reliable claims and explicit authority rather than apparent fluency.

</details>


### [113] [Multimodal Reward Hacking in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09492)
*Jiayu Yao,Yiwei Wang,Anmeng Zhang,Zhe Sun,Songsong Wang,Lingrui Mei,Yuyao Ge,Shenghua Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态大语言模型在强化学习对齐中的奖励破解问题，发现仅依赖结果奖励会导致严重的奖励破解（最高达48.1%），即便扩大模型规模（2B-32B）也无法完全消除，而基于答案的奖励和可靠的视觉验证器能有效缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习越来越多地被用于对齐多模态大语言模型，但更高的奖励并不总是意味着更好的任务表现，尤其是在视觉证据仅由纯文本或弱监督奖励评估时，奖励破解风险被放大。本文旨在系统性地研究多模态MLLM强化学习中的奖励破解现象及其影响因素。

Method: 在安全VQA、图表VQA和压力测试三种设置下，系统变化奖励设计、数据模糊性、模型规模（2B-32B）和强化学习算法（GRPO、RLOO、DAPO），并引入新指标"新增奖励失败率（NRFR）"来量化奖励破解程度。

Result: 仅使用结果奖励时，奖励破解率（RHR）高达48.1%，且NRFR超过RHR表明RL创造了新失败而非仅继承已有失败。扩大模型规模可缓解但无法消除破解，32B模型在仅结果奖励下仍有54.9%的恶化率。GRPO最鲁棒，RLOO最脆弱，DAPO从2B到8B有显著改善。视觉证据奖励仅在具备可靠验证时有效，关键词检查反而增加破解，VLM作为评判者的语义验证能减少破解。

Conclusion: 多模态奖励破解是优化不完美奖励的系统性结果，稳健的对齐需要奖励和验证器在优化压力下仍保持可靠。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to align multimodal large language models (MLLMs), but higher rewards do not always imply better task performance. This risk is amplified when visual evidence is evaluated by text-only or weakly grounded rewards. We study reward hacking in MLLM RL across safety VQA, chart VQA, and stress-test settings, varying reward design, data ambiguity, model scale (2B-32B), and RL algorithm (GRPO, RLOO, DAPO). We introduce Newly Rewarded Failure Rate (NRFR), which measures failures among samples whose proxy reward improves over the SFT baseline. Outcome-only rewards cause severe hacking, reaching 48.1% Reward Hacking Rate (RHR), while NRFR exceeding RHR shows that RL creates new failures rather than merely inheriting them. Scaling reduces but does not eliminate hacking: even the 32B model retains a 54.9% worse rate under outcome-only rewards, whereas answer-aware rewards improve the oracle trend at every scale. Robustness is also algorithm- and scale-dependent: GRPO is consistently most resistant, RLOO remains vulnerable, and DAPO improves substantially from 2B to 8B. Visual-evidence rewards help only with reliable verification: keyword-based checks increase hacking, while VLM-as-judge semantic verification reduces it. Overall, multimodal reward hacking is a systematic result of optimizing imperfect rewards, and robust alignment requires rewards and verifiers that remain reliable under optimization pressure.

</details>


### [114] [Shared Selective Persistent Memory for Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09493)
*Sanjana Pedada,Aditya Dhavala,Neelraj Patil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了共享选择性持久记忆架构，通过保留四类可复用上下文（任务规范、数据模式、工具配置、输出约束）并丢弃会话特定推理轨迹，实现LLM代理在多轮代码生成中的高效协作。配合零令牌数据刷新机制，在三个企业场景中任务完成率达96%，令牌成本降低97倍，14倍任务时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Agentic LLM系统在多轮工具使用中面临上下文缺失问题：每次会话从零开始，丢弃了之前会话中积累的配置选择、领域约束、数据模式及工具使用模式。简单保留完整对话历史存在令牌效率低下且干扰生成质量的问题。

Method: 提出共享选择性持久记忆架构，识别并保留四类可复用上下文（任务规范、数据模式、工具配置、输出约束），丢弃会话特定推理轨迹。记忆通过工作空间封装支持跨用户基于角色的访问控制。设计零令牌数据刷新机制，将生成的程序与运行时数据解耦。实现为部署的协作工作空间平台，支持从CSV、SQL、REST API、MCP服务器生成git版本化文档。

Result: 三个企业场景中：选择性记忆任务完成率96%（无记忆79%，完整历史71%）；零令牌刷新实现14倍任务时间缩减；摘要驱动生成相较原始数据注入降低97倍令牌成本；在四个公开数据集上零令牌刷新12/12次成功。

Conclusion: 共享选择性持久记忆显著优于无记忆和完整历史持久化的两种极端方案，完整历史持久化会因陈旧轨迹偏见主动降低任务完成率。该方法可有效支持跨用户协作复用，并通过零令牌刷新机制大幅降低重复调用的计算开销。

Abstract: Agentic LLM systems that generate code through multi-turn tool use face a fundamental context problem: each session starts from zero, discarding the configuration choices, domain constraints, data schemas, and tool-use patterns that made previous sessions productive. Naively persisting entire conversation histories is token-inefficient and counterproductive: irrelevant context degrades generation quality. We introduce shared selective persistent memory, an architecture that identifies and retains four categories of reusable context (task specifications, data schemas, tool configurations, and output constraints) while discarding session-specific reasoning traces. Crucially, this memory is shared: workspaces encapsulating selective memory can be transferred across users with role-based access control, enabling collaborative reuse without redundant specification. We implement it in a deployed collaborative workspace platform where LLM agents produce, edit, and maintain git-versioned artifacts (dashboards, reports, and data-driven documents) from heterogeneous sources (CSV, SQL, REST APIs, and MCP servers). A complementary zero-token data refresh mechanism decouples generated programs from runtime data, enabling artifact reuse without re-invocation. Across three enterprise scenarios, shared selective persistent memory achieves 96% task completion (vs. 79% without memory and 71% with full history). Zero-token refresh eliminates LLM re-invocation for recurring updates (14x task-time reduction), while summary-driven generation cuts per-invocation token cost by 97x versus raw data injection. A replication on four public datasets confirms generalizability, with zero-token refresh succeeding in 12/12 trials. Notably, naive full-history persistence actively degrades completion by biasing the agent with stale traces, while selective memory outperforms both extremes.

</details>


### [115] [SAGEAgent: A Self-Evolving Agent for Cost-Aware Modality Acquisition in Multimodal Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09521)
*Chongyu Qu,Can Cui,Zhengyi Lu,Junchao Zhu,Tianyuan Yao,Junlin Guo,Juming Xiong,Yanfan Zhu,Yuechen Yang,Bennett A. Landman,Yuankai Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGEAgent，一种基于LLM的临床决策智能体，能够根据患者具体情况决定是否需要获取下一级诊断模态，在保持生存预测精度的同时将平均诊断负担减少55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态生存预测方法要么假设所有模态都可用，要么被动处理缺失数据，但没有方法主动推理对于给定患者沿着有序诊断工作流获取下一模态是否合理。临床实践中诊断模态遵循从低负担到高负担的有序流程，需要平衡预测精度与临床侵入性。

Method: 将多模态获取问题形式化为序贯决策问题，提出SAGEAgent：一种自进化的LLM驱动的临床智能体，利用临床工具将数值预测转化为文本、情景记忆检索相似历史病例、语义记忆积累可复用的决策模式，为每位患者决策获取哪些诊断模态。

Result: 在结合TCGA-LGG、TCGA-GBM和BraTS的胶质瘤队列上，使用四种诊断模态（人口统计学、影像、病理、基因组）的实验表明，SAGEAgent在保持竞争性生存预测精度的同时，将平均获取负担降低了55%。

Conclusion: 主动推理式模态获取策略可以有效减少不必要的诊断负担，LLM结合记忆机制能够实现个体化的临床决策，为精准医疗提供更高效的多模态诊断路径。

Abstract: Does every cancer patient truly need a complete diagnostic workup for accurate survival prediction? In multimodal clinical oncology, diagnostic modalities follow a clinically mandated order of escalating burden -- from demographics collected at intake to genomic profiling requiring specialized tissue analysis. Current multimodal survival methods either assume all modalities are available or passively handle missing data, but none actively reason about whether acquiring the next modality is justified for a given patient along this ordered workflow. We formulate this as a sequential decision problem and propose SAGEAgent (Sequential Acquisition Guided by Experience), a self-evolving LLM-based clinical agent that decides which diagnostic modalities to acquire for each patient, balancing predictive accuracy against clinical invasiveness. SAGEAgent reasons about each patient's evolving diagnostic state through clinical tools that translate numerical predictions into text, an episodic memory that retrieves similar past cases, and a semantic memory that accumulates reusable decision patterns from experience. Experiments on a glioma cohort combining TCGA-LGG, TCGA-GBM, and BraTS with four diagnostic modalities demonstrate that SAGEAgent achieves competitive survival prediction accuracy while reducing average acquisition burden by 55%.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond Fixed Representations: The Vocabulary and Verifier Gaps in Open-Ended AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09560)
*Yuan Cao,Haiqian Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有AI系统受限于固定表征框架，无法实现真正开放式的创新。作者提出两个关键差距：词汇差距（难以创造新的表征基元）和验证差距（难以评估新基元的价值），并通过"认知差异缩减"统一框架区分了框架内变换与生成性变换，最后提出了创新自主性阶梯及推进开放域AI的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统（如推理、编码、定理证明、工具使用等）虽然能力强，但都共享一个结构性限制：模型操作的表征框架（包括概念词汇、可解空间、成功标准）是事先固定提供的。要实现真正的开放式创新，需要能创建、稳定和复用新的表征基元，从而改变正在搜索的空间本身。

Method: 通过两个概念性差距（词汇差距和验证差距）刻画当前AI与真正开放智能之间的距离，并引入"认知差异缩减"统一框架，将智能行为视为认知变换序列，区分"框架内变换"（intra-space transformation）和"生成性变换"（generative transformation）。

Result: 提出了创新自主性阶梯（ladder of innovation autonomy），以及推进开放域AI的若干方向：奖励有用表征变化的目标函数、持久记忆架构用于发明基元、以及自适应验证机制。

Conclusion: 要实现开放域智能，需要超越在固定空间中搜索的范式，赋予AI系统修改自身表征框架的能力，包括词汇创造、价值判断和框架演进等元认知能力。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are increasingly being evaluated for their ability to reason, code, prove theorems, use tools, and long-horizon research tasks. These are powerful capabilities, but they share a structural limitation: the representational frame within which the model operates, including its conceptual vocabulary, the space of admissible solutions it can search, and the criteria by which success is evaluated, is typically fixed and supplied in advance. This paper argues that building stronger intelligent systems capable of open-ended innovation requires additional classes of operations: the creation, stabilization, and reuse of new representational primitives, which alter the space being searched rather than simply searching within it.
  We characterize the distance between current AI systems and genuinely open-ended intelligence through two gaps. The first is the vocabulary gap, the difficulty of inventing and stabilizing new representational primitives rather than merely recombining existing ones. The second is the verifier gap, the difficulty of judging the value of a new primitive when its full payoff may be visible only after future reuse. We interpret both gaps through a unified framework of intelligence as cognitive discrepancy reduction. By viewing intelligent behaviors as a sequence of cognitive transformations, we distinguish intra-space transformations which operate within a fixed representational frame, from generative transformations which may modify the frame itself. On this basis, we propose a ladder of innovation autonomy and outline several directions for advancing open-ended AI, including objectives that reward useful representational change, persistent memory architectures for invented primitives, and adaptive verification mechanisms capable of evolving alongside the representations they evaluate.

</details>


### [117] [Knowledge Graphs and Explainable AI as Complementary Resources for Urban Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09578)
*Jan Gronewald,Andreas Emrich,Nijat Mehdiyev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从互补性理论视角，提出KG-XAI的四种集成模式（提升、约束、类型化、修正），以解决预拆评估中决策的可辩护性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对知识图谱与可解释AI的集成描述丰富但结构不明确，缺乏对"为何特定集成能产生单独资源无法提供的制品"的结构性解释。

Method: 基于IS资源基础观的互补性理论，提出四种KG-XAI集成模式（Lifting, Constraining, Typing, Revising），每种模式定义为对XAI制品和知识图谱基底结构的类型化操作。

Result: 每种集成模式解锁一种独特的可辩护性属性，为预拆评估所需的监管制品提供支持。通过城市采矿中的防火门案例（基于W3C链接建筑数据栈）进行了模式说明。

Conclusion: AI辅助预拆评估的价值单位不仅是预测准确性，更是决策的可辩护性（可读性、合理性、可溯源性、可争议性）；KG-XAI的结构化集成是实现这一目标的可行路径。

Abstract: Pre-demolition assessment, the regulated audit process at the heart of urban mining, is an information process in which AI support must serve qualified auditors who remain accountable for the decisions taken. The relevant unit of value is not prediction accuracy alone, but the defensibility of the supported decisions: their legibility, plausibility, sourcing, and contestability. Explainable AI techniques and domain knowledge graphs each address parts of this requirement, and existing taxonomies have catalogued their integration. The literature is descriptively rich but structurally under-specified: what remains less developed is a structural account of why specific integrations produce artefacts neither resource can provide alone. This paper offers a complementarity-theoretic interpretation grounded in the IS resource-based tradition. We propose four consolidated KG-XAI integration modes (Lifting, Constraining, Typing, and Revising), each defined as a typed operation over XAI artefacts and knowledge-graph substrate structures. Each mode unlocks a distinct property of defensibility and contributes to the kind of regulatory artefact pre-demolition assessment demands. A fire-door example from the urban-mining process illustrates the modes using the W3C Linked Building Data stack and valuation extensions.

</details>


### [118] [TrustX Agent Risk Classification Framework (ARC): Risk-Tiering Internally Created Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09586)
*Hannah M. Liu,Rhea Saxena,Shiv Asthana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TrustX Agent风险分类框架（ARC），这是一个针对代理型AI系统的结构化风险评估工具，基于十二维度评分体系、GPA+IAT分类模型和五级自主性框架，生成三级治理输出并附有控制建议，还包含专门针对编程助手的扩展模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理型AI系统在企业及公共部门的快速普及已超越了通用AI风险框架的分类与治理能力，现有框架难以有效应对这类系统的独特风险特征，亟需一个专门化、可重复的结构化评估工具。

Method: 框架核心包含一个十二维度的评分体系，结合GPA+IAT分类模型和基于文献的五级自主性框架，共同生成三级治理输出（附带控制建议），并针对代码助手场景设计了专门扩展模块。

Result: 通过一个示例展示了框架的实际应用流程，提供了可交互的在线访问地址（https://arc.responsible.ai/），并说明框架将定期迭代以增强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ARC框架面向AI治理从业者、风险官员、开发者和监管机构，为七类代理型AI系统提供了结构化、可重复的风险分类与治理方法，弥补了现有通用框架在代理型AI风险治理方面的空白。

Abstract: The proliferation of agentic AI systems across enterprise and public-sector contexts has outpaced the capacity of general-purpose AI risk frameworks to classify and govern them. In this paper, we introduce the TrustX Agent Risk Classification Framework, a structured, repeatable instrument that can be applied to seven types of agentic AI systems and is grounded in foundational pre-existing AI governance frameworks. At the core of the framework is a twelve-dimension scoring rubric that robustly quantifies the risk. This rubric is combined with other components, such as the GPA + IAT classification model and the five-level autonomy framework derived from existing literature. These inputs produce a three-tier governance output with mapped control recommendations. A specialised Coding Assistant extension is also included to account for nuances specific to this type of agentic AI system. We then use an illustrative example to show our framework in practice. ARC is intended for AI governance practitioners, risk officers, developers, and regulators, and it will regularly undergo iteration as we continue to expand it and make it more robust. The community can access the interactive framework here: https://arc.responsible.ai/

</details>


### [119] [Agora: Enhancing LLM Agent Reasoning Via Auction-Based Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09600)
*Kaiji Zhou,Ales Leonardis,Yue Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agora框架，通过激励相容的拍卖机制动态分配任务给专家模型和工具，将推理步骤视为可交易物品，基于修正后的能力进行竞价，从而提升LLM代理的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架基于任务与专家模型功能的粗粒度匹配调用API，忽略了功能相似替代方案之间的性能差异和成本效率问题。

Method: 提出Agora框架，引入激励相容的拍卖机制，将推理步骤作为可交易物品，让代理基于修正后的能力（rectified competence）进行竞价，确保关键逻辑路由到最有能力的求解器而非最自信的求解器。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，与匹配的单模型、路由和级联基线相比，Agora在可比较的候选池下性能更优，并通过单一拍卖参数暴露了可控的成本-质量权衡。

Conclusion: Agora通过拍卖机制有效解决了专家模型和工具的动态分配问题，实现了性能与成本之间的可控权衡。

Abstract: Enhancing the reasoning capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents requires effective orchestration of diverse expert models and tools. However, existing frameworks typically call APIs based on coarse-grained matching between tasks and the functions of expert models or tools, while overlooking critical factors such as performance variability and cost efficiency among functionally similar alternatives. To address this, we propose Agora, a framework that introduces an incentive-compatible auction mechanism for dynamically allocating tasks to expert models and tools. By treating reasoning steps as tradeable items, Agora enables agents to bid based on their rectified competence-ensuring that critical logic is routed to the most capable solver rather than the most overconfident one. Evaluations across five benchmarks show that Agora improves over matched single-model, routing, and cascade baselines under comparable candidate pools, while exposing a controllable cost-quality trade-off through a single auction parameter.

</details>


### [120] [ConceptSMILE: Auditing the Trustworthiness of Concept-Based Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09649)
*Mohadeseh Mollapour,Koorosh Aslansefat,Zeinab Dehghani,Bhupesh Kumar Mishra,Tejal Shah,Zhibao Mian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConceptSMILE是一个模型无关的、基于扰动的审计框架，用于评估概念级可解释AI（XAI）解释的可靠性。它扩展了SMILE的扰动逻辑，从特征/区域归因审计扩展到人类可理解的概念解释审计。通过在视网膜眼底图像上比较MedSAM视觉概念与VLM语义概念，发现不同概念和路径的可靠性存在差异：MedSAM在空间归因和代理保真度上表现最优（R²=0.8503），而VLM在血管忠实性和特定伪影条件下的稳定性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念级可解释AI虽然能使模型推理更易理解，但概念级输出本身并不自动可信，缺乏独立评估其可靠性的审计框架。

Method: ConceptSMILE采用模型无关的扰动审计方法：扰动输入区域、测量概念响应偏移、应用局部性加权、拟合XGBoost代理模型以近似局部概念行为，并从归因准确性、代理保真度、忠实性、稳定性和一致性五个维度评估可靠性。

Result: 在视网膜眼底图像上，MedSAM途径在空间归因和代理保真度上表现最佳（R²=0.8503, R_w²=0.8465），VLM途径在血管忠实性和某些伪影条件下的稳定性更强。不同概念和路径的可靠性存在显著差异。

Conclusion: ConceptSMILE为概念级XAI提供了独立的审计层，可用于评估其可信度，并揭示了不同概念和解释途径在可靠性上的差异。

Abstract: Concept-based explainable artificial intelligence (AI) can make model reasoning more human-understandable, but concept-level outputs are not automatically trustworthy. We introduce ConceptSMILE, a model-agnostic perturbation-based auditing framework for evaluating the reliability of concept-based explanations. Rather than replacing SMILE, ConceptSMILE extends its perturbation-based logic from feature- or region-level attribution to the auditing of human-understandable concept explanations. The framework perturbs input regions, measures concept-response shifts, applies locality weighting, and fits an XGBoost surrogate to approximate local concept behaviour. Reliability is assessed through attribution accuracy, surrogate fidelity, faithfulness, stability, and consistency. We evaluate ConceptSMILE on retinal fundus images by comparing MedSAM-derived visual concepts with VLM-based semantic concepts. Results show that reliability varies across concepts and pathways: MedSAM achieves stronger spatial attribution and the highest surrogate fidelity ($R^2 = 0.8503$, $R_w^2 = 0.8465$), while the VLM pathway shows stronger vessel faithfulness and stronger stability under selected artefact conditions. ConceptSMILE provides an independent audit layer for evaluating the trustworthiness of concept-based XAI.

</details>


### [121] [SolarChain-Eval: A Physics-Constrained Benchmark for Trustworthy Economic Agents in Decentralized Energy Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08681)
*Shilin Ou,Yifan Xu,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SolarChain-Eval，一个物理约束的基准测试，用于评估去中心化能源市场中可信赖的经济智能体，通过Gymnasium兼容的MDP框架、LLM规划器/审计器层以及多维度评估指标，揭示了效用与安全性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI系统在物理网络环境（如去中心化能源市场）中应用时，可能利用无效物理数据、制造虚假流动性、产生不稳定的治理决策，现有评估缺乏同时衡量任务性能和可信赖性的方法。

Method: 将市场治理建模为Gymnasium兼容的马尔可夫决策过程，智能体按小时决策；设计多维评估指标（市场效用、物理安全性、滑点、动作平滑性、空间公平性、可审计性）；集成LLM规划器定义episode级动作边界和审计规则，LLM审计器审查并修改高风险动作，所有干预通过结构化日志记录。

Result: 静态、随机、短视、强化学习和RL+LLM策略实验表明存在明确的效用-安全性权衡；RL智能体提升市场效用但可能产生不安全行为；移除物理惩罚后，奖励最大化智能体会利用无效发电并增加虚假流动性；LLM规划器/审计器改善了可审计性并缓解了部分风险，但无法完全补偿错误指定的奖励函数。

Conclusion: 可信赖的智能体AI评估需要物理约束和透明的干预轨迹，数据与代码已在GitHub开源以支持可复现性。

Abstract: As agentic AI systems are increasingly applied to cyber-physical environments, their evaluation requires assessment of both task performance and trustworthiness. In decentralized energy markets, autonomous agents may improve market utility, but may also exploit invalid physical data, create artificial liquidity, and produce unstable governance decisions. Therefore, we propose SolarChain-Eval, a physics-constrained benchmark for evaluating trustworthy economic agents. It formulates market governance as a Gymnasium-compatible Markov Decision Process, where agents make hourly decisions. SolarChain-Eval evaluates each policy across multiple dimensions, including market utility, physical safety, slippage, action smoothness, spatial fairness, and auditability. To support agentic evaluation, SolarChain-Eval incorporates an LLM-based Planner/Auditor layer. The Planner defines episode-level action bounds and audit rules, while the Auditor reviews and revises high-risk actions. All interventions are recorded through structured logs, including trigger signals, proposed actions, revised actions, and audit rationales. Experiments with static, random, myopic, RL, and RL+LLM policies reveal a clear utility-safety trade-off. RL agents improve market utility but can still produce unsafe behavior. When the physics penalty is removed, reward-maximizing agents exploit invalid generation and increase artificial liquidity. The LLM Planner/Auditor improves auditability and mitigates selected risks, but it cannot fully compensate for a misspecified reward function. These results indicate that trustworthy agentic AI evaluation requires both physical constraints and transparent intervention traces. We release data and code as open access on GitHub for replicability.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [A Unified Approach to Interpreting Knowledge Distillation for Large Language Models via Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08776)
*Qingzhuo Wang,Ruiyang Qin,Zhenxin Qin,Wen Shen,Zhihua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架，发现各类知识蒸馏方法的共同机制是交互稀疏化，并据此设计复杂交互惩罚（CIP）损失函数来提升蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管知识蒸馏在大语言模型中取得了成功，但其背后的作用机制尚不明确，需要统一的理论解释。

Method: 将LLM的输出分数分解为多个交互（interactions）之和，每个交互代表一组输入变量（如词语）之间的非线性关系。通过分析交互的稀疏化揭示蒸馏共同机制，并据此提出CIP损失函数显式强化复杂交互的稀疏性。

Result: 大量实验表明，CIP损失函数能够持续提升多种知识蒸馏方法在领域内和分布外基准上的性能。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏的本质机制是交互稀疏化（学生模型保留更少交互进行推理），不同蒸馏方法的性能差异源于其对复杂交互的处理能力；CIP损失函数可作为即插即用模块提升蒸馏效果。

Abstract: Despite the success of knowledge distillation (KD) in Large Language Models (LLMs), the underlying mechanism behind its efficacy remains unclear. In this paper, we propose a unified approach to explore the common mechanism of various KD methods using interactions. Specifically, we decompose the output score of the LLM into the sum of numerous interactions. Each interaction represents a nonlinear relationship involving a set of input variables (e.g., words). Based on the decomposed interactions, we discover that the common mechanism underlying various KD methods is the sparsification of interactions, i.e., student models retain fewer interactions for inference while suppressing other interactions to zero effects. Furthermore, we discover that the performance variance across different KD methods arises from their capabilities in handling complex interactions. A KD method typically yields better performance if it enables the student model to achieve higher sparsity of complex interactions. Motivated by these insights, we propose a plug-and-play loss function called Complex Interaction Penalty (CIP) to explicitly enforce the sparsity of complex interactions during the distillation process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that integrating CIP consistently improves the performance of diverse KD methods on both in-domain and out-of-distribution benchmarks.

</details>


### [123] [iLENS: Interpretable LLM-Guided Mixture-of-Experts for Neuroimaging Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08778)
*Farica Zhuang,Seong Woo Han,Zixuan Wen,Shu Yang,Yize Zhao,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iLENS是一个基于大语言模型和混合专家系统的可解释生存预测框架，用于阿尔茨海默病转化预测，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时提供生物可解释的决策依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AD转化生存预测模型是静态的、可解释性有限且缺乏自然语言推理能力，临床决策支持需求与高性能预测之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出iLENS框架，利用大语言模型融合结构化神经影像测量和非结构化信息，通过混合专家系统指导专家路由进行生存预测。

Result: 框架展示了竞争性的预测性能和患者亚型分析能力。

Conclusion: iLENS提供了透明、生物学可解释的路由决策依据，弥合了高性能生存分析与可解释临床决策支持之间的差距。

Abstract: Alzheimer's Disease (AD) is a complex neurodegenerative disorder that continues to impact millions of people worldwide. Predicting AD conversion during the prodromal stage remains critical for disease understanding and patient care. As such, survival models are widely used for AD risk prediction, yet they are typically static predictors with limited interpretability and no capacity for natural language reasoning. In this work, we propose iLENS, an interpretable large language model (LLM) guided framework based on mixture-of-experts (MoE) for survival prediction in AD conversion. Our approach uses LLM to synthesize structured neuroimaging measurements and unstructured information to guide expert routing. Our framework demonstrates competitive predictive performance and capability in patient subtyping. Furthermore, our framework provides transparent, biologically grounded rationales for its routing decisions, bridging the gap between high-performance survival analysis and interpretable clinical decision support.

</details>


### [124] [Signed Symmetric Quantization for Few-Bit Integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08779)
*Ian Colbert,Eashan Dash,Pablo Monteagudo-Lago,Juan Amboage,Srinidhi N,Giuseppe Franco,Nicholas J. Fraser,Arun Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有符号绝对值对称量化（signed absmax grid），通过在保持零点为零的前提下，将额外的一个负表示值分配给占主导的离群值尾端，解决了标准对称量化中因正负表示值不对称导致的裁剪误差问题，同时避免了非对称量化的运行时开销。理论证明该方法在ℓ₂量化误差上条件性地达到边界最优，且该条件在低比特下覆盖88-99%的权重组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对称量化因有符号整数正负表示值不对称（负值比正值多一个），将额外负值分配给负尾可能导致正离群值被裁剪，产生显著量化误差；非对称量化虽可通过零点偏移解决该问题，但会带来运行时开销（如llama.cpp中4-bit对称格式比非对称节省最多9%内存且吞吐量高2.45倍）。因此需要一种兼顾低误差与低开销的量化方案。

Method: 提出有符号绝对值对称量化（signed absmax grid），核心是轻量级的符号选择规则：将额外的一个负表示值分配给离群值占主导的尾端，同时保持零点为零。通过理论分析证明该网格在ℓ₂量化误差上是条件边界最优的，并证明将标准对称量化器的尺度取反等价于在有符号整数字母表上做单位零点偏移。

Result: 在预训练大语言模型（Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Llama3系列）的权重分组中，88-99%满足该量化网格的条件边界最优性；在实际部署中，该方法在无额外推理开销的情况下，相比标准无符号对称量化提升了困惑度和少样本准确率。

Conclusion: 有符号绝对值对称量化是一种兼顾对称量化的高效运行时特性与非对称量化的低误差优势的可行方案，特别适用于低比特精度下大语言模型的量化部署。

Abstract: The signed integer alphabet contains one more negative representable value than positive. Yet, by convention, the standard symmetric integer quantizer fixes its scale to be strictly positive, which assigns this extra representable value to the negative tail and can force clipping of positive outliers. In this work, we show that, at few-bit precision, such clipping is a non-trivial source of quantization error. Asymmetric quantization addresses this problem with a zero point, shifting the grid toward the observed data range; however, this flexibility is well-known to carry a runtime penalty. For example, in llama.cpp on an AMD EPYC(TM) "Turin" CPU, a 4-bit symmetric format uses up to 9% less memory with up to 2.45$\times$ higher throughput than its asymmetric counterpart. We highlight signed symmetric quantization as a third option that retains the runtime profile of symmetric quantization without the penalty of the asymmetric format: our signed absmax grid places the extra representable value on the dominant-outlier tail through a principled and lightweight sign selection rule while keeping the zero point at zero. Our theoretical analysis offers two main results. First, we establish the signed absmax grid as conditionally bound-optimal on $\ell_2$ quantization error, and show that the condition holds for 88-99% of weight groups across pre-trained large language models (LLMs) at low bit widths. Second, we show that negating the scale of a standard symmetric quantizer is analytically equivalent to a unit zero point shift on the same signed integer alphabet. We empirically validate our proposal on models from the Qwen3, Qwen3.5, and Llama3 families, and observe improvement in perplexity and downstream few-shot accuracy over the standard unsigned symmetric quantizer at no extra inference cost

</details>


### [125] [Sticky Routing: Training MoE Models for Memory-Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08780)
*Ali Kayyam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StickyMoE通过可微分的路由一致性损失函数，在预训练阶段鼓励相邻token使用相同专家，从而减少MoE模型在边缘设备上的专家切换开销，最高可降低60%的切换率，困惑度增加不到4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型中连续token频繁激活不同专家，导致边缘设备上权重在慢速存储和快速内存之间不断交换，造成严重效率瓶颈。现有方案（系统级缓存启发式、后处理路由微调）均为治标之策，未在预训练阶段解决根本原因。

Method: 提出StickyMoE，引入可微分的路由一致性损失函数，对相邻token之间的专家切换行为进行惩罚，鼓励路由在语义连贯的片段内保持相同的专家分配。该方法无需改变模型架构，仅增加一个超参数lambda，且在预训练第一步起即可让专家表示与路由决策共同适应。

Result: 在小规模MoE语言模型上，StickyMoE将专家切换率降低高达60%，困惑度退化小于4%，在质量-局部性帕累托前沿上优于后处理微调方法。

Conclusion: 路由的时间局部性在训练阶段注入最为高效，StickyMoE以极简方式实现了对MoE部署效率的根本性改进。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models activate only a sparse subset of experts per token, yet consecutive tokens frequently activate different experts -- causing constant weight swapping between slow storage and fast memory on edge devices. Existing remedies are either system-level (caching heuristics) or post-hoc (router fine-tuning), leaving the root cause unchanged during pretraining. We propose StickyMoE, a differentiable routing consistency loss that penalises abrupt expert switches between adjacent tokens, encouraging the router to maintain the same expert assignment across semantically coherent spans. StickyMoE requires no architectural changes, adds a single hyperparameter lambda, and unlike post-hoc methods, allows expert representations and routing decisions to co-adapt from the first training step. Experiments on small-scale MoE language models show that StickyMoE reduces the expert switch rate by up to 60% with less than 4% perplexity degradation, Pareto-dominating post-hoc fine-tuning on the quality-locality frontier. Routing temporal locality is most efficiently instilled at training time.

</details>


### [126] [Reward Transport: Property Control in Flow Matching via Noise-Space Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08781)
*Kehan Guo,Yili Shen,Yujun Zhou,Yue Huang,Chujie Gao,Shiyi Du,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reward Transport方法，通过最优传输耦合在流匹配中将噪声与数据按分子属性对齐，实现在推理时通过调节标量坐标控制分子生成属性，无需奖励模型或额外计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配中的耦合（噪声向量与数据点的配对规则）通常仅被视为计算选择，但本文旨在证明耦合可作为对齐接口，利用目标分子属性将可控结构嵌入学习到的流场中。

Method: 采用最优传输耦合在训练时将标量噪声空间坐标与分子奖励对齐；推理时通过改变该标量坐标引导生成分布，无需预言机、奖励模型、梯度引导或额外计算。在耦合保持极限下，对该坐标进行阈值化可恢复交叉熵方法的截断奖励分布。

Result: 在ZINC-250K和GuacaMol上，扫描该标量可实现对logP的单调控制和QED在其操作范围内的一致控制；对于不同目标，同一控制旋钮产生相反的结构响应（logP使分子变大，QED使分子变小），排除了通用尺寸偏差。

Conclusion: 该界面与无分类器引导和条件流匹配互补；在epsilon预测扩散下的负面结果阐明了耦合级对齐在结构上缺失的情况。

Abstract: The coupling in flow matching -- the rule pairing noise vectors with data points -- is typically treated as a computational choice. We show that this coupling can instead serve as an alignment interface: by matching noise and data according to a target molecular property, it embeds controllable structure directly into the learned flow field. Building on this view, we introduce Reward Transport, which uses optimal transport coupling at training time to align a scalar noise-space coordinate with molecular rewards; at inference, varying this coordinate steers the generated distribution without requiring an oracle, reward model, gradient guidance, or additional computation. In the coupling-preserving limit, thresholding this coordinate recovers the Cross-Entropy Method's truncated reward distribution, providing a principled, continuously adjustable distribution-level control knob. Empirically, on ZINC-250K and GuacaMol, sweeping the scalar induces monotone control of logP and consistent QED control over its operating range; most tellingly, the same knob produces opposite structural responses for different targets, growing molecules for logP but shrinking them for QED, which rules out a generic size bias. The interface is complementary to classifier-free guidance and conditional flow matching, while a negative result under epsilon-prediction diffusion clarifies where coupling-level alignment is structurally absent. Code: https://github.com/KehanGuo2/reward-transport

</details>


### [127] [Director: Accelerating Distributed MoE Serving via Online Proactive Expert Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08782)
*Qianli Liu,Kaibin Guo,Zicong Hong,Peng Li,Fahao Chen,Haodong Wang,Jian Lin,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Director是一个面向MoE模型的新型分布式推理系统，通过预测驱动的在线专家放置优化，在多项式时间内实现(1+ε)近似比，端到端延迟降低11~55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专家放置优化方法依赖历史请求的专家激活模式，面对多样化和快速变化的请求模式时效果不佳，需要一种在线、主动的放置方法。

Method: 采用轻量级级联预测器或低比特量化副本预测请求的专家激活模式；在线迁移模块在计算密集型阶段执行迁移以实现近零停机；基于松弛优化的专家放置器在容量约束下运行于多项式时间并达到(1+ε)近似比。

Result: 在Mistral、DeepSeek、Qwen等主流MoE模型上，相比现有工作，端到端延迟减少11~55%。

Conclusion: 证明了预测驱动的在线专家放置方法能有效应对多样化请求模式，显著提升MoE服务系统的推理效率。

Abstract: Expert parallelism has become the prevailing paradigm to serve Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. Its efficiency depends on the communication and computation latencies of the GPUs, which are linked to the placement of experts in the GPUs. Existing works for optimizing expert placement focus on leveraging past requests' expert activation patterns. However, they demonstrate deficiencies facing diverse and rapidly changing request patterns, calling for an online, proactive approach. Implementing such an approach requires addressing several challenges: the uncertainty associated with incoming requests' expert activation, the cost of expert migration, and the NP-hard complexity in optimization. Therefore, we present Director, a new distributed MoE serving system that minimizes end-to-end latency via prediction-driven, online expert placement. Director uses either a lightweight cascaded predictor or a low-bit quantized replica for expert activation patterns of incoming requests. An online migration module then enacts the changes with near-zero downtime by executing migrations in compute-bound phases, keeping disruption bounded. At its core, a relaxation-based expert placement optimizer operates under capacity constraints, runs in polynomial time, and achieves a $(1+ε)$ approximation ratio. Finally, we implement a prototype and demonstrate, through extensive experiments, a reduction in end-to-end latency of $11\sim55\%$ for popular MoE models (e.g., Mistral, DeepSeek and Qwen) compared to existing work.

</details>


### [128] [LieBN: Batch Normalization over Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08783)
*Ziheng Chen,Yue Song,Rui Wang,Xiao-Jun Wu,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LieBN框架，一种基于李群的黎曼批归一化方法，利用左/右不变度量控制黎曼均值和方差，在9种几何结构上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黎曼归一化方法要么针对特定流形设计，要么无法有效归一化流形值样本分布，缺乏通用且理论保证的框架。

Method: 提出LieBN框架，利用李群上天然存在的左/右不变度量，提供控制黎曼均值和方差的理论保证。在SPD流形上引入新的右不变度量，并通过矩阵幂变形扩展三种李群结构，在9种几何上实例化。

Result: 在9种不同几何结构（4种SPD度量、旋转矩阵群、4种满秩相关矩阵流形）上实验验证有效性。

Conclusion: LieBN框架有效解决了现有方法的局限性，为李群上的批归一化提供了通用且理论完备的解决方案。

Abstract: Manifold-valued measurements are prevalent in various machine learning tasks. Recent advances have extended Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) to operate on manifolds, accompanied by normalization techniques tailored to different geometries, collectively referred to as Riemannian normalization. However, most existing Riemannian normalization methods are either designed for specific manifolds or fail to effectively normalize manifold-valued sample distributions. To address these limitations, we propose LieBN, a framework for Riemannian Batch Normalization (RBN) over Lie groups. Our approach leverages the theoretically convenient left- and right-invariant metrics, which naturally exist in every Lie group, and provides theoretical guarantees for controlling the Riemannian mean and variance. We instantiate LieBN across nine distinct geometries: four on the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) manifold, one on the group of rotation matrices, and four on the manifold of full-rank correlation matrices. Notably, among the SPD metrics, we introduce a novel right-invariant metric and extend three existing Lie group structures via matrix power deformation. Extensive experiments on different manifolds validate the effectiveness of our framework. The code is available at https://github.com/GitZH-Chen/LieBN.git.

</details>


### [129] [HERO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Benchmark Library for Federated Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08784)
*Thinh T. H. Nguyen,Le-Tuan Nguyen,Minh-Duong Nguyen,Nhi Trinh,Anh Tran Nam Nguyet,Dung D. Le,Kok-Seng Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HERO基准库，通过解耦任务划分、客户端数据划分和客户端任务序列三个维度，实现联邦持续学习的标准化可比评估，揭示了方法在不同异构设置下的行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦持续学习评估因同时变更数据集、任务划分、客户端数据划分、任务顺序、骨干网络、内存假设和报告规则而难以比较，亟需一个解耦可控的标准化基准来支持可重复和设置感知的评估。

Method: 设计HERO基准库，将常耦合的三个选择分离：任务划分、客户端数据划分和客户端任务序列。HERO-Core中通过α控制客户端数据偏斜，ρ控制任务顺序不匹配。在CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet上评估，使用最终平均准确率、平均遗忘率和底部10%客户端准确率。并包含基于图域的Domain-IL案例研究。

Result: 方法行为在简单与异构设置下存在差异；平均准确率可能掩盖底部客户端性能差的问题；任务顺序不匹配与同步评估偏好不同策略；HERO接口可暴露超越图像FCIL的域偏移难度。

Conclusion: HERO提供了基准流、配置、方法实现和报告脚本，支持可重复且设置感知的FCL评估，揭示了现有评估中容易被忽视的异构性影响。

Abstract: Federated continual learning (FCL) evaluates how distributed clients learn from changing data streams while retaining previously learned knowledge. Existing evaluations are difficult to compare because they often change datasets, task splits, client data splits, task orders, backbones, memory assumptions, and reporting rules simultaneously. We introduce \textbf{HERO}, a heterogeneity-aware benchmark library for FCL. HERO builds benchmark streams by separating three choices that are often coupled, namely the task split, the client data split, and the client task sequence. In HERO-Core, the main comparable benchmark, $α$ controls client data skew and $ρ$ controls task-order mismatch. We evaluate representative FCL methods on CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet using final average accuracy, average forgetting, and bottom-10\% client accuracy. We also include a graph-based Domain-IL portability case study on OGB-MolPCBA, where scaffold-domain granularity changes the input distribution while the prediction task remains fixed. Our results show that method behavior changes across easy and heterogeneous settings, that average accuracy can hide weak bottom-client performance, that task-order mismatch favors different strategies from synchronized evaluation, and that the same HERO interface can expose domain-shift difficulty beyond image-based FCIL. HERO releases benchmark streams, configurations, method implementations, and reporting scripts to support reproducible and setting-aware FCL evaluation.

</details>


### [130] [DaDaDa: A Dataset for Data Pricing in Data Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08785)
*Qiheng Sun,Hongwei Zhang,Junxu Liu,Xiaokai Mao,Jinfei Liu,Kui Ren,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DaDaDa数据集，包含来自9个主要数据市场的16,147个数据产品元数据，用于解决数据产品定价缺乏标准化基准的问题，支持定价模型训练、分类和检索任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据交易日益普遍，但由于数据产品具有零边际成本和收入不可预测等特性，传统定价方法（成本法、收益法）失效，而市场比较法又缺乏标准化定价基准，因此亟需一个统一的数据产品定价基准数据集。

Method: 构建DaDaDa数据集，收集来自9个全球主要数据市场平台的16,147个数据产品的元数据；基于该数据集训练定价模型，建立新数据产品的价格基准；并开发检索原型系统。

Result: 实验表明DaDaDa在数据产品定价、分类和检索任务上均有效。

Conclusion: DaDaDa作为首个数据产品定价数据集，能够有效建立定价基准，并支持数据市场的其他重要任务（分类与检索），数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: High-quality data drives machine learning advances across industries. Recognizing the value of data, data transactions are increasingly common, giving rise to many data marketplaces, e.g., AWS Marketplace, Databricks, and Datarade. However, determining the appropriate prices for data products remains a significant challenge due to the unique properties of data products. Traditional pricing methods in economics can be categorized into the cost approach, the income approach, and the sales comparison approach. The cost approach fails in data pricing due to near-zero marginal cost from data replication, and the income approach fails due to inherently unpredictable data revenue. The sales comparison approach remains viable, yet its application is hindered by the absence of standardized pricing benchmarks for data products across marketplaces. To address this challenge, we introduce \texttt{DaDaDa}, the first dataset for data product pricing, containing metadata for 16,147 data products from 9 major data marketplaces worldwide. \texttt{DaDaDa} enables the training of pricing models, thereby establishing price benchmarks for new data products. In addition, \texttt{DaDaDa} can be utilized for other important tasks in data markets, such as data product classification and retrieval. Experiments and a retrieval prototype demonstrate the effectiveness of \texttt{DaDaDa} for pricing, classification, and retrieval of data products. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/ZJU-DIVER/DaDaDa.

</details>


### [131] [Accelerating GPU Inference of Large Language Models with Moderately Unstructured Sparse Weight Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08786)
*Tao Lu,Haoyu Wang,Zonghui Wang,Keshen Xiang,Jiaheng Zhang,Wenzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对中等稀疏度LLM权重的GPU高效推理方法。通过设计三层矩阵存储格式（Sparse-TC层、Slot-Filling层、Residual层）并联合利用稀疏张量核与CUDA核的SpMM内核，首次在配备HBM的现代GPU上超越稠密矩阵乘法，实现最高1.64x内核级加速和1.41x端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署规模增长，推理成本成为关键挑战。剪枝技术可在权重矩阵中引入稀疏性来加速推理，但为保持模型质量，通常只能达到约50%的中等非结构化稀疏度。在此稀疏度水平下，现有GPU稀疏矩阵乘法（SpMM）内核均无法超越稠密矩阵乘法，亟需新的高效推理方法。

Method: 提出三层矩阵存储格式：(i) Sparse-TC层——利用稀疏张量核加速SpMM；(ii) Slot-Filling层——采用并行差分距离进行矩阵压缩，支持低代价片上解码；(iii) 轻量级Residual层——确保正确SpMM计算。基于此格式设计联合利用稀疏张量核与CUDA核的SpMM内核，实现高效执行流水线并重叠片上计算与内存访问。

Result: 首次在配备HBM的现代GPU上超越稠密矩阵乘法，内核级较SpInfer（EuroSys'25最佳论文）加速最高1.64x，端到端较FlashLLM（VLDB'24）加速最高1.41x。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了中等非结构化稀疏度下SpMM性能瓶颈，证明了通过合理设计存储格式与异构计算核协同可以超越稠密矩阵乘法，为LLM推理加速提供了新路径。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of large language models (LLMs), LLM inference cost has become a key challenge. Pruning techniques that introduce sparsity into weight matrices can accelerate inference. However, maintaining model quality typically limits pruning to moderate unstructured sparsity (around 50\%). At these sparsity levels, none of the existing GPU kernels for sparse matrix multiplication (SpMM) can outperform their dense counterparts. This paper proposes an efficient GPU inference method for LLMs with moderate sparsity. We propose a three-layer matrix storage format comprising: (i) a Sparse-TC layer enabling sparse tensor cores to accelerate SpMM; (ii) a Slot-Filling layer using parallel differential distance for matrix compression while supporting low-cost on-chip decoding; (iii) a lightweight Residual Layer ensuring correct SpMM computation. Building on this format, we design a SpMM kernel that jointly utilizes sparse tensor cores and CUDA cores. This design enables an efficient execution pipeline and overlaps on-chip computation with memory access. Evaluations show that our work is the first to outperform dense matrix multiplication on modern GPUs equipped with high-bandwidth memory (HBM). It achieves up to 1.64x kernel-level speedup over SpInfer (EuroSys'25, Best paper) and up to 1.41x end-to-end speedups over FlashLLM (VLDB'24). Our source code: https://github.com/moui0/cudac.

</details>


### [132] [Adaptive Bayes exactly tracks information over intrinsic time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08789)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，贝叶斯更新和乘法权重更新的遗憾（regret）满足一个精确的信息会计恒等式：每一轮学习者的超额损失等于不确定性支付的即时成本与到比较器的信息距离减少量之和。该路径化分解适用于Hedge、乐观变体、在线凸优化、上下文赌博机等多种算法，且随机或低噪声场景通过自约束性质自然呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗憾分析多为上界形式的近似或松弛，缺乏精确的恒等式来揭示更新规则与遗憾之间的内在信息论关系。作者希望建立一种统一的、精确的遗憾分解框架，使得不同算法和场景下的遗憾行为可以用同一个路径化信息会计恒等式来解释。

Method: 从贝叶斯和乘法权重更新的信息论视角出发，推导出每个回合的遗憾等于"不确定性支付"（暴露当前不确定性的即时成本）与"信息距离缩减量"（到比较器的权重距离减少）之和。将累积支付定义为路径化的"内在时间"，并给出两种自然合成方式的精确自适应遗憾分解。

Result: 得到了精确的遗憾信息会计恒等式，该等式不是上界而是精确等式。在随机或低噪声场景中，内在时间的自约束性质自然体现了优势，而非最坏情况分析中的松弛项。该框架统一覆盖了Hedge、乐观变体、连续先验、Boosting、在线凸优化、上下文赌博机和重复博弈等广泛场景。

Conclusion: 揭示了贝叶斯和乘法权重更新的遗憾本质上由路径化的信息论量精确刻画，为理解和分析在线学习算法的性能提供了统一且精确的理论工具。

Abstract: Bayesian and multiplicative-weights updates reweight experts, models, or actions from sequential feedback. We show that the regret of any such update obeys an exact information-accounting identity. On each round, the learner's excess loss to any chosen comparator is the sum of an immediate payment for the uncertainty exposed by the round and a reduction in the information distance from the learner's current weights to the comparator. The cumulative payment defines a pathwise uncertainty clock, the \emph{intrinsic time} of the realized sequence. Summing one-step balances yields two exact adaptive decompositions of cumulative regret, one for each natural way of composing the update across rounds. Because the decompositions are exact rather than upper bounds, favorable stochastic or low-noise regimes appear as self-bounding properties of the realized intrinsic time, not as slack in worst-case analyses. The same calculus covers Hedge, optimistic and side-information variants, continuous priors, boosting, online convex optimization, contextual bandits, and repeated games: the pathwise account is the same in every case.

</details>


### [133] [Prompt-Driven Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08837)
*Sunshine Jiang,John Marangola,David Zhang,Raghuram Kowdeed,Ruiyang Luo,Nitish Dashora,Richard Li,Pulkit Agrawal,Zhang-Wei Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Prompt-Driven Exploration (PDE)方法，利用视觉语言模型(VLM)分析策略执行视频并重写自然语言提示，实现提示层面的后验采样探索，从而在强化学习中从零奖励起始学习成功策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL探索方法仅在动作空间添加噪声，无法产生全局性扰动以逃离弱策略；当策略成功率极低时，奖励过于稀疏无法用于选择有用提示。

Method: 使用VLM对策略执行的回放视频进行分析，诊断策略响应方式，然后重写自然语言提示以引导出更好的行为；该方法在提示层面实现了后验采样（posterior sampling）这一经典RL探索框架。

Result: 在操作任务和推理任务上，PDE使RL能够从零奖励起始学习到成功策略，并广泛提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: 提示驱动的探索方法能有效克服稀疏奖励下的探索难题，为结合大语言模型/视觉语言模型与强化学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Exploration is essential to RL since a policy cannot improve by repeatedly sampling the behaviors it already prefers. Standard methods inject stochasticity in the action space, but such jitter only yields rollouts close to the original. Escaping a weak policy often requires global perturbations that action noise cannot produce. Large language models (LLMs) and vision-language-action (VLA) models offer a pathway: they condition the policy on a natural language prompt, and since the rollout follows from it, modifying the prompt induces global changes. The challenge is finding prompts that induce useful global changes. With a weak policy that rarely succeeds, reward is too sparse to select on. Our idea is to refine prompts from the rollouts themselves: a vision-language model (VLM) reasons over the rollout video, diagnoses how the policy responded, and rewrites the prompt to elicit better behavior next time. This procedure realizes posterior sampling, a classical RL exploration framework, at the level of prompts: the VLM maintains an implicit distribution over useful prompts and updates it from observed rollouts. We call this strategy Prompt-Driven Exploration (PDE). Across manipulation and reasoning tasks, PDE enables RL to learn successful policies even from zero-reward starts, and improves sample efficiency more broadly. Our website is available at https://xinyunsunshine.github.io/prompt-rl.

</details>


### [134] [How are linear representations learned? Exact solutions to the dynamics of abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08843)
*William W. Yang,Andrew M. Saxe,Peter E. Latham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经网络训练过程中概念方向（线性表征）的对齐动态过程，即"抽象化"。在线性网络中推导了完整解析解，揭示了控制抽象化的三个关键原理：数据与目标几何共同决定最终抽象程度、网络深度提升抽象效果、初始化尺度控制训练中最大抽象水平。对非线性网络的分析表明：erf网络近似线性理论，ReLU网络的抽象更依赖输入几何而非目标几何，且非线性会削弱激活层的抽象性。该理论在DINOv3、Gemma 4等开源模型中得到验证，并可用于提升LLM线性探针的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注训练后线性方向是否存在，但对训练过程中这些方向如何涌现的动态过程尚不清楚。本文旨在建立一套理论框架来研究训练过程中概念方向的对齐动态（即"抽象化"），填补这一空白。

Method: 在线性最小网络中推导抽象化完整轨迹的解析解，从中提炼关键原理；然后将理论扩展到非线性网络（erf和ReLU），分析非线性函数对抽象动态的影响；最后在开源模型（DINOv3、Gemma 4）中验证理论预测，并应用于提升LLM线性探针的泛化能力。

Result: 揭示了三个控制抽象化的核心原理：数据与目标几何联合决定最终抽象程度、网络深度提升抽象效果、初始化尺度控制训练中最大抽象水平。发现了非线性衰减定律：erf和ReLU两种非线性都会削弱激活层相对于预激活层的抽象程度，并在DINOv3和Gemma 4模型中得到验证。

Conclusion: 本文建立了神经网络训练过程中抽象化的动态理论，为可解释性和控制方法提供了理论基础，并可实际应用于改进LLM线性探针的泛化性能。

Abstract: In artificial and biological neural networks, concepts are often encoded as consistent linear directions in representation space. In deep learning, this idea is known as the linear representation hypothesis and underpins many interpretability and control methods based on linear probes, from concept detection to activation steering. Yet while prior work has studied whether such directions should exist $\textit{after}$ training, the dynamics of how they emerge $\textit{during}$ training remain poorly understood. Here, we develop a framework to study the alignment of concept directions during training - a process we call "abstraction". In a minimal linear network setting, we obtain exact solutions for the full trajectory of abstraction. These solutions reveal key analytic principles governing abstraction: (i) data and target geometry jointly determine abstraction at the end-of-learning, (ii) abstraction improves with network depth, and (iii) initialization scale controls the maximum abstraction reached during training. Extending our theory to nonlinear networks, we analyze how the choice of nonlinearity affects abstraction dynamics: erf networks approximate the linear theory, while abstraction in ReLU networks depends less on target geometry and more on input geometry. Across both, we prove a striking attenuation law: both nonlinearities weaken abstraction in activations relative to preactivations. We find evidence for this law in open models (DINOv3, Gemma 4) and apply our theory to improve linear probe generalization in LLMs. Together, our results provide a dynamical theory of abstraction with implications for interpretability and control.

</details>


### [135] [Optimizing Against Safety Representations: Activation-Guided Adversarial Suffixes and the Geometry of Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08883)
*Ege Çakar,Hannah Guan,Kayden Kehe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型中的安全表示并非因果定位于单一位置，而是分布在整个前向传播过程中。作者提出了Activation-Guided GCG方法，通过直接针对模型内部拒绝方向进行攻击，并发现全局抑制比局部定位更有效。此外，Soft-GCG方法使用Gumbel-Softmax连续松弛离散后缀优化，实现了33倍加速。实验表明，较小模型仍易受攻击，而较大模型在计算受限条件下更具抵抗力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型中行为对齐的脆弱性——安全表示是如何结构化、定位以及被优化过程访问的。具体而言，研究对抗性后缀攻击如何作为表征对齐的探针，揭示安全机制的本质。

Method: 1) 提出Activation-Guided GCG，用直接靶向模型内部拒绝方向的损失替代基于输出的目标；2) 比较全局抑制与单层-位置对抑制的效果；3) 提出Soft-GCG，利用Gumbel-Softmax实现离散后缀优化的连续松弛，加速优化过程。

Result: 1) 全局层和位置的安全表示抑制比单点定位更有效；2) Soft-GCG相比标准GCG实现33倍加速且攻击成功率更高；3) 较小模型仍然脆弱，较大模型在计算受限设置下能够抵抗两种攻击。

Conclusion: 安全表示在模型中分布而非局部化，较大且经过更好安全训练的模型更难被越狱。这些发现为设计更鲁棒且具有表示感知的对齐策略提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Behavioral alignment in large language models often masks fragile internal safety representations. Recent work suggests that refusal behavior is mediated by low-dimensional directions in activation space. This raises questions about how such representations are structured, localized, and accessed by optimization. We study adversarial suffix attacks as a probe of representational alignment. We introduce Activation-Guided GCG, which replaces output-based objectives with losses that directly target a model's internal refusal direction. Across several objective variants, we find that suppressing refusal globally across all layers and positions is more effective than targeting a single layer-position pair. This suggests that safety representations are distributed across the forward pass rather than causally localized to a single site. We further introduce Soft-GCG, a continuous relaxation of discrete suffix optimization using Gumbel-Softmax. Soft-GCG achieves a 33 $\times$ speedup over standard GCG while improving attack success rates. Evaluating across model scales, we find that smaller models remain vulnerable while larger models resist both activation- and suffix-based attacks at our compute-constrained settings, consistent with larger and better safety trained models being harder to jailbreak. Together, our results clarify how safety mechanisms are encoded and can be broken in contemporary models. These insights provide concrete guidance for designing more robust and representation-aware alignment strategies.

</details>


### [136] [Pattern-Aware Graph Neural Networks for Handling Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08915)
*Minett Tran,Taehee Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模式感知图神经网络，通过显式编码缺失模式来处理现实数据中的非随机缺失问题。在七个UCI数据集上，该方法相比基线在平衡准确率上平均提升17%，F1-macro提升22%。研究表明，区分缺失模式比任务特定优化更重要，简单的随机嵌入即可达到与学习嵌入相近的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据中普遍存在缺失值，传统方法要么丢弃不完整样本，要么进行插补，但都假设缺失是随机的，忽略了缺失模式本身可能包含有用信息。

Method: 提出模式感知图神经网络，采用四种编码策略（学习嵌入、冻结随机嵌入、统计特征、层次表示）显式编码缺失特征及缺失模式，结合图神经网络进行学习。

Result: 在七个UCI自然缺失数据集上，平衡准确率平均提升17%，F1-macro平均提升22%。不同数据集提升差异显著（退火数据集+80%，肝炎和大豆仅+4-5%）。随机嵌入（0.650）与学习嵌入（0.663）表现相近。简单均值聚合（0.640）与注意力变体（0.645）效果接近。

Conclusion: 缺失模式编码能有效提升模型性能，区分缺失模式比任务特定优化更为关键；注意力机制并非必要，简单的模式感知聚合即可取得良好效果。

Abstract: Missing data is ubiquitous in real-world datasets. Traditional methods either discard incomplete samples or apply imputation techniques that ignore potentially informative missingness patterns, implicitly assuming that missingness occurs randomly. However, missingness patterns might provide additional information. We propose pattern-aware graph neural networks that explicitly encode which features are missing alongside observed values. We used four encoding strategies -- learned embeddings, frozen random embeddings, statistical features, and hierarchical representations -- across seven UCI datasets with naturally occurring missingness. Our Pattern-aware methods achieve substantial improvements over baselines, with an average improvement of 17\% in balanced accuracy and 22\% in F1-macro across all datasets. The benefits vary significantly by dataset: annealing shows dramatic improvement (+80\% balanced accuracy), while hepatitis and soybean show minimal gains (+4--5\%). Notably, even simple random pattern embeddings perform comparably to learned embeddings (0.650 vs 0.663 balanced accuracy), suggesting that distinguishing between patterns may be more important than task-specific optimization. Our ablation study reveals that attention mechanisms, while helpful, are not critical when pattern information is available -- simple mean aggregation with pattern awareness achieves 0.640 balanced accuracy compared to 0.645 for attention-based variants.

</details>


### [137] [A Machine Learning Surrogate for Component Criticality Ranking in Interdependent Power-Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08918)
*Sohini Roy,Xheni Hylviu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对电力-通信耦合系统的连锁故障评估计算开销大的问题，提出了一种基于梯度提升的机器学习替代模型，利用无泄漏的结构特征预测N-k事故严重性并推导组件关键性排序。在IEEE 118节点系统上，替代模型的Spearman相关性达0.849（事故级）和0.853（组件级），达到MIIM本身采样上限（约0.85），支持“替代模型快速排序+MIIM选择性验证”的两阶段工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力-通信耦合系统易因基础设施间的紧密依赖而发生连锁故障。使用高保真模拟器评估大规模N-k事故集的计算成本过高，阻碍了韧性规划的开展。

Method: 以改进隐式依赖模型(MIIM)为地面真值模拟器，提取无泄漏的结构特征，训练梯度提升(GB)替代模型用于预测事故严重性并推导组件关键性排序。

Result: 在IEEE 118节点系统上，GB替代模型对事故严重性预测的Spearman相关系数为0.849，组件关键性排序为0.853；MIIM自身在该采样流程下仅达约0.85；拓扑中心性基线为0.60-0.69；特征消融实验表明优势主要来自层间依赖信息。

Conclusion: 替代模型达到了MIIM采样的经验上限，支持两阶段工作流：替代模型快速排序候选组件，MIIM用于选择性验证。

Abstract: Cyber-physical power systems are vulnerable to cascading failures caused by tight interdependencies between power and communication infrastructures. Evaluating these failures over large N-k contingency sets with a high-fidelity simulator is computationally prohibitive for resilience planning. Using the previously published Modified Implicative Interdependency Model (MIIM) as the ground-truth cascade simulator, this paper develops a machine-learning surrogate that predicts contingency severity from leakage-free structural features and derives a component-criticality ranking for prioritized hardening analysis. On the IEEE 118-bus system, the Gradient Boosting surrogate achieves Spearman correlations of 0.849 for per-contingency severity prediction and 0.853 for per-component criticality ranking, while remaining stable across three independently sampled datasets. MIIM-derived component criticality itself reproduces only to a Spearman of approximately 0.85 under the present sampling pipeline, and the surrogate operates at this empirical ceiling to within sampling variation. Topological centrality measures on the full interdependent network provide meaningful baselines (Spearman 0.60-0.69), and feature ablation shows that the surrogate's advantage is driven primarily by inter-layer dependency information. These results support a two-stage workflow in which the surrogate rapidly ranks candidate components and MIIM is reserved for selective verification.

</details>


### [138] [SafeExplorer: An Unbiased Policy Gradient for Reinforcement Learning with Recovery Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08925)
*Elham Daneshmand,Majid Khadiv,Glen Berseth,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无偏PPO修改方法，在训练期间使用安全恢复策略减少机器人摔倒，通过仅在安全时间步使用评分函数避免重要性采样偏差，并引入两种加速组件，在多个环境中将训练摔倒次数降低26-233倍，同时保持或超越原始PPO的最终奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理机器人上的强化学习训练中每次摔倒代价高昂，无法像仿真中那样重置，因此需要最小化训练期间的摔倒次数。现有的混合策略更新存在偏差，重要性采样修正又在恢复策略为确定性时失效。

Method: (1) 无偏策略梯度估计器：仅在安全时间步使用评分函数，从不评估恢复策略的密度，避免重要性采样偏差； (2) 封闭形式值函数：当动力学和恢复策略均为确定性时，为触发恢复的状态提供精确值； (3) 模仿损失：仅当恢复成功时复制恢复策略的动作，加速安全边界附近的信用分配。

Result: 在HalfCheetah、Ant和Unitree Go1三个环境中，相比标准PPO，训练摔倒次数分别减少233倍、48倍和26倍，同时最终奖励持平或超越；在恢复策略不可靠的Ant环境中，是唯一达到最佳最终奖励80%的方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效减少物理机器人训练中的摔倒次数，解决混合策略更新偏差问题，并在恢复策略不可靠时仍能保持良好性能。

Abstract: Training reinforcement-learning agents directly on physical robots makes every fall costly, since a fall can damage the platform and cannot be undone like a simulator reset; the goal is therefore to minimize falls during training rather than trade them off against return, as constrained Markov decision process (MDP) formulations do. A standard mitigation hands control to a separate recovery policy whenever the agent leaves a designer-specified safe region (a subset of state space it should stay within), but the resulting mixed-policy rollouts silently bias every on-policy update, and the importance-sampling correction that would remove this bias is ill-defined whenever the recovery policy is deterministic. We address this bias with a drop-in modification of proximal policy optimization (PPO). Its core is an unbiased policy-gradient estimator that uses the score function only at safe timesteps and never evaluates the recovery policy's density, so it stays valid even when the recovery policy is deterministic, exactly where importance sampling breaks, and it empirically dominates importance sampling even when the recovery policy is stochastic. Because the recovery policy still makes credit assignment slow near the safe-region boundary, two further components accelerate learning: a closed-form value for recovery-triggering states when dynamics and recovery are deterministic, and an imitation loss that copies recovery actions only when recovery succeeds. On a three-environment, five-seed benchmark, the resulting algorithm reduces training-time falls by factors of 233x, 48x, and 26x on HalfCheetah, Ant, and Unitree Go1 over standard PPO, while matching or exceeding PPO's final reward, and on Ant, where the recovery policy is unreliable, it is the only method that reaches 80% of the best final reward.

</details>


### [139] [BlockServe: Block-Grained Continuous Batching for High-Throughput Diffusion LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08930)
*Yuanjie Zhu,Liangwei Yang,Ke Xu,Weizhi Zhang,Shanghao Li,Zihe Song,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BlockServe通过块粒度调度与混合状态执行，解决扩散大语言模型服务中的收敛异构性问题，在Dream和LLaDA上实现1.9-10.6倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型(dLLM)在批量服务时存在收敛异构性问题：不同序列收敛速度不同，导致快请求被慢请求阻塞，产生计算气泡和尾部延迟。

Method: 1) 块粒度连续批处理调度：在块边界立即驱逐完成请求；2) 混合状态执行：通过gather-scatter索引将双缓存和并行解码扩展到异构批处理；3) 计算感知的准入控制器：通过token预算的补充机制扩展有效批容量。

Result: 在Dream和LLaDA上五个基准测试中，相比Fast-dLLM实现1.9-10.6倍吞吐量提升，且生成质量相当。

Conclusion: 块粒度调度可作为高吞吐离线dLLM推理的基础框架。

Abstract: Efficient serving of diffusion large language models (dLLMs) is hindered by convergence heterogeneity: when batching multiple requests, different sequences converge at different rates, causing faster requests to stall behind slower stragglers and introducing compute bubbles and tail latency. We present BlockServe, a continuous batching framework that integrates block-grained scheduling -- immediately evicting completed requests at block boundaries -- with mixed-state execution that extends dual cache and parallel decoding to heterogeneous batches via gather-scatter indexing. Furthermore, a compute-aware admission controller expands effective batch capacity through token-budgeted refill. On Dream and LLaDA across five benchmarks, BlockServe achieves 1.9--10.6$\times$ throughput over Fast-dLLM with comparable generation quality, establishing block-grained scheduling as a foundation for high-throughput offline dLLM inference.

</details>


### [140] [TSRouter: Dynamic Modality-Model Selection for Time Series Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08940)
*Fangxu Yu,Tao Feng,Dehai Min,Lu Cheng,Ge Liu,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TSRouter，一种基于图结构的动态路由框架，用于在时间序列推理任务中根据查询特性动态选择最优的模态（文本/图像）和模型组合，在4种任务上取得16%-46%的相对提升，并支持零样本泛化到未见模型和新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM处理时间序列能保持精确数值理解但难以捕捉全局模式，VLM虽能高效捕捉全局模式但可能丢失细粒度细节，且不同模型在任务专长和推理成本上差异显著。因此需要动态选择最合适的模态和模型组合，但这一过程需要建模任务、查询、模态和模型之间的复杂交互关系。

Method: TSRouter构建一个包含任务、查询、模态和模型节点的异构图，对查询特征、模态属性和模型能力之间的交互关系进行上下文化建模。将路由选择形式化为候选评分问题，基于用户定义的性能-成本偏好对每个模态-模型对进行评分，从而选择最优候选。

Result: 在4种不同的时间序列推理任务上，TSRouter相比多种基线方法取得16%到46%的相对提升。同时展现出对未见模型和新任务的鲁棒零样本即插即用泛化能力，并通过成本感知优化在降低计算开销的同时保持高性能。

Conclusion: TSRouter通过图结构动态路由框架有效解决了时间序列推理中模态和模型选择问题，兼顾性能与成本，具有良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Time series reasoning is essential for real-world problem-solving. While both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can reason about time-series data, their capabilities are complementary: LLMs process time series as text sequences and thus preserve exact numerical understanding, but struggle with global patterns, whereas VLMs efficiently capture these patterns by visualizing time series but may lose fine-grained details. Moreover, models vary significantly in task-specific expertise and inference costs. Dynamically selecting the most suitable modality and model for each query is therefore crucial, yet challenging because it requires modeling the complex interactions among tasks, queries, modalities, and models, which carry rich contextual signals. To this end, we introduce TSRouter, a graph-based dynamic routing framework. TSRouter constructs a heterogeneous graph of task, query, modality, and model nodes to contextualize the interactions among query characteristics, modality attributes, and model capabilities. TSRouter formulates routing as a candidate scoring problem, where each modality-model pair is evaluated based on user-defined performance-cost preferences to select the optimal candidate. Comprehensive evaluations on 4 distinct time series reasoning tasks reveal that TSRouter substantially outperforms diverse baselines with 16\% to 46\% relative improvements. Furthermore, TSRouter demonstrates robust zero-shot plug-and-play generalization to unseen models and novel tasks and preserves high performance while reducing computational overhead through cost-aware optimization. Our code is available at https://github.com/tianyi-lab/TSRouter.

</details>


### [141] [Training, Reading, and Editing Legible Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08946)
*Mark Oskin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过可解性惩罚和方差约束构建高度可解释Transformer的方法，使得78%前馈算子和50%注意力通道成为清晰可辨的检测器，同时保持与基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer的激活值密集且不透明，难以解释和编辑；先前工作通过预留GELU分区实现部分可解性，但依赖手工设置且效率低下。

Method: 1) 引入清晰度惩罚（crispness penalty）但发现其会导致方差最小化而使单元坍缩为常数；2) 基于恒等式 E[v(1-v)] = μ(1-μ) - Var，提出按通道的方差下界作为可解性损失；3) 学习逐单元分数以替代手工设置的预留GELU分区；4) 引入单元间去相关压力作为可解性调节旋钮。

Result: 1) 模型未保留任何纯GELU单元，87%承载计算通过清晰算子完成；2) 78%前馈算子和50%注意力值通道为清晰且上下文相关的检测器；3) 逐头可解性从浅层的18%提升至深层的78%；4) 深层编辑的局部性提升50-184倍；5) 去相关压力可在不损失质量的前提下将概念转化为单一可编辑单元。

Conclusion: 通过恰当的损失设计，可以在不牺牲性能的前提下构建高度可解释的Transformer，使模型内部操作从密集激活转变为类似模糊集运算的清晰命名单元，并支持局部编辑和简短解释。

Abstract: A transformer can be built from operators that are legible by construction -- bounded, named units that read as fuzzy set operations rather than dense activations -- but legibility must be pressed for during training, and the pressure has a failure mode. A crispness penalty meant to sharpen a bounded operator into a decisive detector instead collapses it into a dead constant. An identity, E[v(1-v)] = mu(1-mu) - var, shows why -- the penalty is a variance-minimizer blind to the difference between a live detector and a constant -- and names the fix: a per-channel variance floor, the target legibility metric written as a loss, which recovers both legibility and quality. A learned per-unit fraction then retires the hand-set reserved-GELU partition of prior work: given the choice the model keeps no unit as pure GELU and routes 87% of its load-bearing computation through crisp operators. The result is the most legible transformer we have built -- 78% of its feed-forward operands and 50% of its attention value channels are crisp-and-contextual detectors, and per-head legibility rises from 18% in shallow layers to 78% in deep ones. Read in the correct rotated per-layer frame, these units separate a clean detection (what a unit responds to) from a harder naming (what its output decodes to); and because the objective makes each unit crisp and sparse, edits to them are far more local -- 50-184x in the deep layers where the edit sites concentrate -- and can target explicit conjunctions a single neuron cannot express. Finally, a between-unit decorrelation pressure exposes a legibility dial: it trades a circuit's reuse for independence at no quality cost, turning concepts into single, surgically editable units and a prediction into a short explanation read off a handful of named operations. Quality holds at parity with a conventional baseline throughout.

</details>


### [142] [FairSelect: A Systematic Evaluation of Multi-Level and Intersectional Algorithmic Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08953)
*Nick Souligne,Isabella Mixton-Garcia,Vignesh Subbian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairSelect是一个用于系统性评估机器学习公平性缓解策略的工具包，支持在预处理、处理中、后处理阶段单独或组合应用多种方法，并通过合成和真实临床数据验证发现公平性干预存在非加性且依赖上下文的交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性方法大多在孤立条件下沿单一人口统计学轴评估，缺乏跨交叉子群和建模生命周期多阶段的实用指导，难以有效选择公平性策略。

Method: 开发FairSelect工具包，支持预处理、处理中、后处理阶段的单独及组合公平性缓解策略评估，涵盖多种模型架构、交叉子群评估和公平性-效用权衡比较，并通过合成临床数据集和真实房颤患者卒中风险预测任务进行验证。

Result: 合成实验表明针对性公平性方法可减少目标子群差异，组合策略带来更大的平均公平性提升但伴随适度效用权衡；临床预测中缓解效果高度变异，部分组合同时改善公平性和预测性能，另一些则无效或适得其反。

Conclusion: 公平性干预存在非加性和依赖上下文的交互效应，FairSelect为系统性识别提升子群公平性同时保持模型性能的公平性策略提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Algorithmic fairness methods are increasingly used to identify and mitigate bias in machine learning models, yet most approaches are evaluated in isolation and along single demographic axes. This limits practical guidance for selecting fairness strategies, where disparities may arise across intersectional subgroups and across multiple stages of the modeling lifecycle. This work presents FairSelect, a toolkit for systematically evaluating fairness mitigation strategies applied individually and in combination across preprocessing, inprocessing, and postprocessing stages. FairSelect supports multiple model architectures, intersectional subgroup evaluation, and comparison of fairness utility tradeoffs across baseline, single method, and multi level configurations. The framework was validated using synthetic clinical datasets designed to represent specific bias mechanisms and a real-world replication of two-year stroke risk prediction among patients with atrial fibrillation. Synthetic experiments showed that targeted fairness methods generally reduced intended subgroup disparities, while combined strategies produced larger average fairness improvements with modest utility tradeoffs. In the clinical prediction task, mitigation effects were highly variable, with some combinations improving both fairness and predictive performance while others were ineffective or counterproductive. These findings demonstrate that fairness interventions interact in nonadditive and context dependent ways. FairSelect provides a practical framework for systematically identifying fairness strategies that improve subgroup equity while preserving model performance in clinical machine learning.

</details>


### [143] [Eluna: An Agentic LLM System for Automating Warehouse Operations with Reasoning and Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08960)
*Ning Liu,Kalle Kujanpää,Zhaoxuan Zhu,P Aditya Sreekar,Kaiwen Liu,Chuanneng Sun,Jorge Marchena Menendez,Matthew Bales,Tianyu Yang,Shahnawaz Alam,Rose Yu,Baoyuan Liu,Kristina Klinkner,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Eluna是一个生产部署的图引导多智能体系统，通过有向无环图编码SOP并渐进式披露，结合非对称情节性蒸馏（强教师+弱学生微调），在仓库操作任务中达到94%的专家一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体缺乏强制执行程序合规性的机制，且完整SOP规范会导致上下文过载，降低性能。

Method: 构建图引导的多智能体框架，将SOP编码为有向无环图（DAG）并渐进式披露；使用非对称情节性蒸馏：强教师通过情节错误记忆改进，小学生在去除记忆的修正轨迹上微调。

Result: 在13任务基准和两个生产应用中，微调模型匹配或超越教师模型，优于所有更大的现成基线，在工单处理应用中达到94%的专家一致性。

Conclusion: Eluna框架有效提升了LLM智能体在复杂SOP执行中的可靠性和准确性，既满足生产环境的延迟要求，又避免了推理时的额外开销。

Abstract: Warehouse operations are governed by Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs) that encode complex, multi-system decision logic, which must be executed reliably under strict time constraints, yet LLM agents lack mechanisms to enforce procedural compliance and degrade under the context overload full SOP specifications introduce. We present Eluna, a production-deployed agentic system for reliable SOP execution. Eluna is a graph-guided, multi-agent framework that encodes SOPs as directed acyclic graphs with progressive disclosure and delegates independent tasks to parallel sub-agents, each with persistent code execution and live data access. To meet production latency and accuracy needs, we use asymmetric episodic distillation where a strong teacher is improved through episodic error memories, then a smaller student is fine-tuned on the corrected trajectories with memory stripped, internalizing corrections without inference-time overhead. On a 13-task benchmark and two production applications, our fine-tuned models match or exceed their teacher, beat all larger off-the-shelf baselines, and reach 94% expert agreement on the ticket processing application.

</details>


### [144] [NL-PAC: Specification Ambiguity and Certified Minimax Risk Floors in LLM-Mediated Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08961)
*Berkay Anahtarci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NL-PAC框架，证明当语言模型通过自然语言规范提供标签时，因规范可多重解读导致的识别问题无法通过增加标注量解决。框架利用固定模型的阈值解码律定义可接受标签集，发现多个标签可接受的概率等于逐点可接受目标类的直径，且在目标盲监督下，任何学习者在任意样本量下的最坏风险至少为该直径的一半。在Qwen2.5-3B模型上的审计实验表明，特定提示可产生正向的模型相对证书，而改写提示和精确规则控制则产生零证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
